COLLOCATION · 高频搭配

高考英语高频搭配速记手册

一词一组 · 按义项分组 · 词块整体记忆
拾词 · 高考英语核心词资料包
电子版 · 下单后免费下载 · 可打印 A4
自研仿真内容，不含真题原文
使用说明：本资料为电子版，可直接在浏览器打印（Ctrl/Cmd + P）为 PDF 或纸质版；双面打印亦可。例句与解析均为自研仿真写作，用于语境记忆训练。

正文 · 高频搭配（按义项分组）

access/ˈækses/T1
考场判断法：① 先看后面是介词 to 还是直接跟宾语：access to sth = 「接近…的机会／使用…的权利」，这是高考最常考的搭配，必须整块记 have access to education / have access to information；② access 后直接跟 system / database / network / file，是「访问、进入」的技术义；③ access 后接 resources / facilities / services，是「使用、获取」；④ 出现 equal access、access to education 这类社会话题时，access 一律是「机会、权利」，不是「通道」。

通道、入口；进入的途径

access road 动词搭配
A narrow access road leads to the farmhouse behind the hill.
一条狭窄的进场路通向山后的农舍。
provide access 动词搭配
The library provides access to thousands of e-books for students.
图书馆为学生提供数千本电子书的访问渠道。
the only access 名词词块
The staircase is the only access to the top floor.
这段楼梯是通往顶层的唯一通道。
access to the building 动词+介词
Visitors need a badge for access to the building.
访客需要胸牌才能进入这栋楼。

接近…的机会；使用…的权利

have access to 动词+介词
Children living in remote areas should all have access to quality medical care.
居住在偏远地区的儿童都应当有机会获得优质的医疗服务。
get access to 动词+介词
Children living in remote areas should all have access to quality medical care.
居住在偏远地区的儿童都应当有机会获得优质的医疗服务。
give sb access to 动词+介词
Children living in remote areas should all have access to quality medical care.
居住在偏远地区的儿童都应当有机会获得优质的医疗服务。
equal access to 动词+介词
Children living in remote areas should all have access to quality medical care.
居住在偏远地区的儿童都应当有机会获得优质的医疗服务。

访问、进入（系统或数据库）

access the Internet 动词搭配
Only registered users can access the database after the system is upgraded.
系统升级后，只有注册用户才能访问该数据库。
access data 动词搭配
Only registered users can access the database after the system is upgraded.
系统升级后，只有注册用户才能访问该数据库。
access a file 动词搭配
Only registered users can access the database after the system is upgraded.
系统升级后，只有注册用户才能访问该数据库。
be accessed by 系表搭配
Only registered users can access the database after the system is upgraded.
系统升级后，只有注册用户才能访问该数据库。

使用、获取（资源与设施）

access resources 动词搭配
Students should be able to access the books and equipment they need in the school library.
学生应当能够使用他们在学校图书馆需要的书籍和设备。
access facilities 动词搭配
Students should be able to access the books and equipment they need in the school library.
学生应当能够使用他们在学校图书馆需要的书籍和设备。
access services 动词搭配
Students should be able to access the books and equipment they need in the school library.
学生应当能够使用他们在学校图书馆需要的书籍和设备。
be easily accessed 系表搭配
Students should be able to access the books and equipment they need in the school library.
学生应当能够使用他们在学校图书馆需要的书籍和设备。
account/əˈkaʊnt/T1
考场判断法：① account for 是高考超高频词块，必须整块记，后面跟百分数（account for 40%）是「占比」，跟名词短语（account for the delay）是「解释原因」；② 看到 the / this + account of（a detailed account of）→ 「记述、描述」；③ 看到 bank / online / open an account → 「账户」；④ on account of = 因为，不是「关于账户」，这个介词短语最容易误读。

（account for）占（比例）、构成

account for 40% 动词+介词
Rent accounts for 40% of my monthly expenses.
房租占我每月开支的40%。
account for a third of 动词+介词
Exports account for a third of the country’s total income.
出口占这个国家总收入的三分之一。
account for the largest share 动词+介词
Health care accounts for the largest share of the budget.
医疗支出占预算的最大份额。
which accounts for 动词+介词
Sales rose in June, which accounts for the higher profit.
六月销售额上升，这就是利润更高的原因。

（account for）解释、说明原因

account for the delay 动词+介词
The storm is thought to account for most of the damage to the old bridge.
人们认为风暴是旧桥大部分损毁的原因。
account for the difference 动词+介词
The storm is thought to account for most of the damage to the old bridge.
人们认为风暴是旧桥大部分损毁的原因。
this accounts for 动词+介词
The storm is thought to account for most of the damage to the old bridge.
人们认为风暴是旧桥大部分损毁的原因。
how do you account for 动词+介词
The storm is thought to account for most of the damage to the old bridge.
人们认为风暴是旧桥大部分损毁的原因。

记述、描述（an account of）

a detailed account of 名词词块
The book gives a vivid account of the writer's years as a volunteer.
这本书生动地记述了作者当志愿者的那些年。
give an account of 动词+介词
The book gives a vivid account of the writer's years as a volunteer.
这本书生动地记述了作者当志愿者的那些年。
an eyewitness account 名词词块
The book gives a vivid account of the writer's years as a volunteer.
这本书生动地记述了作者当志愿者的那些年。
a full account of 名词词块
The book gives a vivid account of the writer's years as a volunteer.
这本书生动地记述了作者当志愿者的那些年。

账户、账目

bank account 动词搭配
She transferred the money to her online bank account before having lunch.
她在吃午饭前把钱转到了自己的网上银行账户。
open an account 动词搭配
She transferred the money to her online bank account before having lunch.
她在吃午饭前把钱转到了自己的网上银行账户。
account balance 动词搭配
She transferred the money to her online bank account before having lunch.
她在吃午饭前把钱转到了自己的网上银行账户。
online account 动词搭配
She transferred the money to her online bank account before having lunch.
她在吃午饭前把钱转到了自己的网上银行账户。

（on account of）因为、由于

on account of 介词短语
Several paths along the river were closed on account of the heavy rain.
河边几条道路由于大雨被封闭了。
on account of the weather 介词短语
Several paths along the river were closed on account of the heavy rain.
河边几条道路由于大雨被封闭了。
solely on account of 动词+介词
Several paths along the river were closed on account of the heavy rain.
河边几条道路由于大雨被封闭了。
address/əˈdres/T1
考场判断法：① 动词后面接 issue / problem / concern / gap / challenge 这类「问题类」名词 → 必是「处理、解决」义；② 接 audience / conference / meeting 这类「人、场合」→ 才是「演讲」义；③ 出现 deliver an address 或 address sb as → 分别是「致辞」与「称呼」；④ 整句是「请留下地址」这类行政语境 → 名词「地址」义。写作里表达「解决问题」优先用 address the problem，这是高考官方搭配，不要用 solve、handle 去顶替。

处理、解决（问题、差距、隐患）

address a problem 动词搭配
We must address a problem before it turns into a crisis.
我们必须在问题演变成危机前处理它。
address the issue 动词搭配
The principal called a meeting to address the issue of late arrivals.
校长开会处理迟到问题。
address a concern 动词搭配
The letter was written to address a concern raised by parents.
这封信是为回应家长提出的担忧而写的。
address the challenge 动词搭配
New technology helps us address the challenge of climate change.
新技术帮助我们应对气候变化的挑战。

向…发表演说；向…讲话

address an audience 动词搭配
She addressed the teachers' conference that afternoon on the importance of early education.
那天下午她在教师大会上就早期教育的重要性发表了演说。
address a meeting 动词搭配
She addressed the teachers' conference that afternoon on the importance of early education.
那天下午她在教师大会上就早期教育的重要性发表了演说。
address the conference 动词搭配
She addressed the teachers' conference that afternoon on the importance of early education.
那天下午她在教师大会上就早期教育的重要性发表了演说。
deliver an address 动词搭配
She addressed the teachers' conference that afternoon on the importance of early education.
那天下午她在教师大会上就早期教育的重要性发表了演说。

称呼、称谓（叫作某称呼）

address sb as 动词搭配
How do you address a colleague from another country you have just met?
你该怎么称呼一位刚认识的外国同事？
the form of address 名词词块
How do you address a colleague from another country you have just met?
你该怎么称呼一位刚认识的外国同事？
address sb by name 动词+介词
How do you address a colleague from another country you have just met?
你该怎么称呼一位刚认识的外国同事？

演讲、致辞（发表的演说）

deliver an address 动词搭配
She addressed the teachers' conference that afternoon on the importance of early education.
那天下午她在教师大会上就早期教育的重要性发表了演说。
give an address 动词搭配
He delivered a moving address to the graduates at the end of the ceremony.
典礼结束时，他向毕业生们发表了一场动人的致辞。
a closing address 名词词块
He delivered a moving address to the graduates at the end of the ceremony.
典礼结束时，他向毕业生们发表了一场动人的致辞。
the opening address 名词词块
He delivered a moving address to the graduates at the end of the ceremony.
典礼结束时，他向毕业生们发表了一场动人的致辞。

地址；门牌号；收件地址

home address 动词搭配
Please leave your address and phone number on the form before you leave.
离开前请在表格上填好你的地址和电话号码。
email address 动词搭配
Please leave your address and phone number on the form before you leave.
离开前请在表格上填好你的地址和电话号码。
change of address 动词+介词
Please leave your address and phone number on the form before you leave.
离开前请在表格上填好你的地址和电话号码。
return address 动词搭配
Please leave your address and phone number on the form before you leave.
离开前请在表格上填好你的地址和电话号码。
appeal/əˈpi:l/T1
考场判断法：① 先看后面接什么：appeal to sb to do sth → 「呼吁、请求」；appeal for sth（help / funding）→ 「请求、募求」；appeal to the court / appeal against a decision → 「上诉」；② 注意同一个 to 的两种含义：appeal to young readers = 「吸引」，appeal to the court = 「上诉」；③ 名词 have / has appeal for sb = 「吸引力」，wide appeal = 受广泛欢迎；④ 名词 an appeal for help 是「呼吁」，不要读成「吸引力」。

呼吁、恳求、请求

appeal to sb to do sth 动词+介词
The teacher appealed to the students to keep the classroom clean.
老师呼吁学生保持教室整洁。
appeal for help 动词+介词
The stranded hikers appeal for help by lighting a fire.
被困的徒步者点起火来求助。
appeal to the government 动词+介词
Local farmers appealed to the government for financial support.
当地农民向政府请求经济援助。
make an appeal 动词搭配
The lawyer decided to make an appeal against the court’s decision.
律师决定对法院的判决提出上诉。

吸引力、感染力

have appeal for sb 动词+介词
The story has a wide appeal, which explains why it was adapted for television.
这个故事极有吸引力，这解释了它为什么被改编成电视剧。
have appeal with sb 动词+介词
The story has a wide appeal, which explains why it was adapted for television.
这个故事极有吸引力，这解释了它为什么被改编成电视剧。
wide appeal 动词搭配
The story has a wide appeal, which explains why it was adapted for television.
这个故事极有吸引力，这解释了它为什么被改编成电视剧。
immediate appeal 动词搭配
The story has a wide appeal, which explains why it was adapted for television.
这个故事极有吸引力，这解释了它为什么被改编成电视剧。

吸引（某人）；让人产生兴趣

appeal to sb 动词+介词
Bright colours and simple designs appeal strongly to young children in the city.
鲜艳的颜色和简洁的设计对城市里的幼儿很有吸引力。
appeal to the taste of 动词+介词
Bright colours and simple designs appeal strongly to young children in the city.
鲜艳的颜色和简洁的设计对城市里的幼儿很有吸引力。
appeal strongly to 动词+介词
Bright colours and simple designs appeal strongly to young children in the city.
鲜艳的颜色和简洁的设计对城市里的幼儿很有吸引力。

上诉、申诉

appeal to the court 动词+介词
He appealed to the court, claiming that the decision had been unfair.
他向法院提起上诉，声称裁决不公。
appeal against a decision 动词+介词
He appealed to the court, claiming that the decision had been unfair.
他向法院提起上诉，声称裁决不公。
lodge an appeal 动词搭配
He appealed to the court, claiming that the decision had been unfair.
他向法院提起上诉，声称裁决不公。
appeal against a verdict 动词+介词
He appealed to the court, claiming that the decision had been unfair.
他向法院提起上诉，声称裁决不公。

呼吁、请求（名词用法）

an appeal for help 名词词块
The charity made a public appeal for food and warm clothes before the winter.
该慈善机构在入冬前公开发出呼吁，募集食物和御寒衣物。
a public appeal 名词词块
The charity made a public appeal for food and warm clothes before the winter.
该慈善机构在入冬前公开发出呼吁，募集食物和御寒衣物。
make an appeal for 动词+介词
The charity made a public appeal for food and warm clothes before the winter.
该慈善机构在入冬前公开发出呼吁，募集食物和御寒衣物。
answer an appeal 动词搭配
The charity made a public appeal for food and warm clothes before the winter.
该慈善机构在入冬前公开发出呼吁，募集食物和御寒衣物。
assumption/əˈsʌmpʃn/T1
考场判断法：① 看到 assumption，先看介词：on the assumption that / on this assumption 一律译成「在…的前提下」，本身带「若…则」的假设语气；② 后面接 responsibility / power / office → 「承担、接管」，绝不译成「假定」；③ 只有 make an assumption、a false assumption 这类抽象用法才是「假定、假设」。另记 -sumption 词族：consumption（消耗）是形近误选重灾区。

假定、假设（未加证实的前提）

make an assumption 动词搭配
Never make an assumption without checking the facts first.
在核实事实之前不要做假设。
a reasonable assumption 名词词块
It is a reasonable assumption that the train will arrive on time.
火车准点到达是一个合理的假设。
the basic assumption 名词词块
The basic assumption of the theory has been questioned.
这个理论的基本假设受到了质疑。
an unwarranted assumption 名词词块
His conclusion rested on an unwarranted assumption.
他的结论建立在一个毫无根据的假设上。

承担、接管（责任、权力、职位）

assumption of responsibility 动词+介词
Her assumption of responsibility for the project changed the way the whole team worked.
她承担起这个项目的责任，改变了整个团队的做事方式。
assumption of power 动词+介词
Her assumption of responsibility for the project changed the way the whole team worked.
她承担起这个项目的责任，改变了整个团队的做事方式。
assumption of office 动词+介词
Her assumption of responsibility for the project changed the way the whole team worked.
她承担起这个项目的责任，改变了整个团队的做事方式。

在…的前提下（固定搭配）

on the assumption that 介词短语
He signed the form on the assumption that the company would pay for the training.
他是在「公司会承担培训费用」这一前提下签了那张表。
on this assumption 介词短语
He signed the form on the assumption that the company would pay for the training.
他是在「公司会承担培训费用」这一前提下签了那张表。
under the assumption that 介词短语
He signed the form on the assumption that the company would pay for the training.
他是在「公司会承担培训费用」这一前提下签了那张表。
bear/beə/T1
考场判断法：① 看到 bear in mind 就是「牢记」，后面常跟 that 从句，不是「忍受」；② 宾语是 responsibility / duty / burden → 「承担」义，是作文高频；宾语是 pain / pressure / consequences → 「承受」义；③ 宾语是 a child / a son → 「生育」义；④ 三态 bear–bore–borne 必须记牢，bore 在「生育、产下」义里还会作为过去式出现。

承受、忍受（痛苦、压力、后果）

bear the pain 动词搭配
She bore the pain bravely without saying a word.
她一声不吭地勇敢忍受着疼痛。
bear the consequences 动词搭配
Anyone who breaks the rule must bear the consequences.
任何违规的人都必须承担后果。
bear the pressure 动词搭配
Young athletes learn to bear the pressure of competition.
年轻运动员学会承受比赛的压力。
bear with me 动词+介词
Bear with me a moment while I find the right file.
请稍等一下，我找一下正确的文件。

承担（责任、义务）

bear the responsibility 动词搭配
Every citizen should bear part of the responsibility for protecting the environment.
每位公民都应承担保护环境的部分责任。
bear responsibility for 动词+介词
Every citizen should bear part of the responsibility for protecting the environment.
每位公民都应承担保护环境的部分责任。
bear the duty 动词搭配
Every citizen should bear part of the responsibility for protecting the environment.
每位公民都应承担保护环境的部分责任。
bear part of the cost 动词+介词
Every citizen should bear part of the responsibility for protecting the environment.
每位公民都应承担保护环境的部分责任。

牢记、把…记在心里

bear in mind 动词+介词
Parents should bear in mind that the early years shape a child's love of learning.
父母应牢记，幼年时光会影响孩子对学习的热爱。
bear in mind that 动词+介词
Parents should bear in mind that the early years shape a child's love of learning.
父母应牢记，幼年时光会影响孩子对学习的热爱。
bear it in mind 动词+介词
Parents should bear in mind that the early years shape a child's love of learning.
父母应牢记，幼年时光会影响孩子对学习的热爱。

生育、产下（孩子）

bear a child 动词搭配
The young couple are expecting their first child and will bear a daughter next spring.
这对年轻夫妇正期待第一个孩子的到来，明年春天将迎来一个女儿。
bear a son 动词搭配
The young couple are expecting their first child and will bear a daughter next spring.
这对年轻夫妇正期待第一个孩子的到来，明年春天将迎来一个女儿。
bear children 动词搭配
The young couple are expecting their first child and will bear a daughter next spring.
这对年轻夫妇正期待第一个孩子的到来，明年春天将迎来一个女儿。
give birth to 动词+介词
The young couple are expecting their first child and will bear a daughter next spring.
这对年轻夫妇正期待第一个孩子的到来，明年春天将迎来一个女儿。

熊（一种大型哺乳动物）

polar bear 动词搭配
Brown bears spend the winter sleeping in quiet caves in the north.
棕熊在北方的安静洞穴里过冬。
bear habitat 动词搭配
Brown bears spend the winter sleeping in quiet caves in the north.
棕熊在北方的安静洞穴里过冬。
grizzly bear 动词搭配
Brown bears spend the winter sleeping in quiet caves in the north.
棕熊在北方的安静洞穴里过冬。
bear population 动词搭配
Brown bears spend the winter sleeping in quiet caves in the north.
棕熊在北方的安静洞穴里过冬。
capacity/kəˈpæsəti/T1
考场判断法：① 先看 capacity 前面有没有 the 加上「人」：the capacity to do sth 是「做…的能力」，读成「容量」就全错；② 看到 in the capacity of sb 一律译成「以…的身份」，这是语法填空的固定考点，不能直译成「有能力」；③ 出现 full / seating / storage 是「容量」，出现 production 是「产能」，三者不可混读。

能力、才能（做某事的本领）

the capacity to do sth 名词词块
Humans have the capacity to learn from their mistakes.
人类有能力从错误中学习。
have the capacity for 动词+介词
She has the capacity for hard work and deep thought.
她有勤奋工作和深入思考的能力。
human capacity 动词搭配
The machine far exceeds human capacity for calculation.
这台机器远远超过人类的计算能力。
a capacity for learning 名词词块
Children are born with a remarkable capacity for learning.
孩子天生具有非凡的学习能力。

容量、容积（能容纳的量）

seating capacity 动词搭配
The new stadium has a seating capacity of tens of thousands, far more than the old one in the city centre.
这座新体育场可容纳数万名观众，比市中心那座旧馆多得多。
storage capacity 动词搭配
The new stadium has a seating capacity of tens of thousands, far more than the old one in the city centre.
这座新体育场可容纳数万名观众，比市中心那座旧馆多得多。
at full capacity 介词短语
The new stadium has a seating capacity of tens of thousands, far more than the old one in the city centre.
这座新体育场可容纳数万名观众，比市中心那座旧馆多得多。
the capacity of a bottle 名词词块
The new stadium has a seating capacity of tens of thousands, far more than the old one in the city centre.
这座新体育场可容纳数万名观众，比市中心那座旧馆多得多。

以…的身份、资格（充当…的身份）

in the capacity of 介词短语
He accepted the invitation to speak in the capacity of the school's headmaster.
他以校长的身份接受了演讲邀请。
in his capacity as 介词短语
He accepted the invitation to speak in the capacity of the school's headmaster.
他以校长的身份接受了演讲邀请。
in an official capacity 介词短语
He accepted the invitation to speak in the capacity of the school's headmaster.
他以校长的身份接受了演讲邀请。

产能、生产能力（系统可产出的规模）

production capacity 动词搭配
The company is expanding its production capacity so that it can meet the rising demand of overseas buyers.
公司正在扩大生产能力，以便满足海外买家不断增长的需求。
expand capacity 动词搭配
The company is expanding its production capacity so that it can meet the rising demand of overseas buyers.
公司正在扩大生产能力，以便满足海外买家不断增长的需求。
idle capacity 动词搭配
The company is expanding its production capacity so that it can meet the rising demand of overseas buyers.
公司正在扩大生产能力，以便满足海外买家不断增长的需求。
capacity building 动词搭配
The company is expanding its production capacity so that it can meet the rising demand of overseas buyers.
公司正在扩大生产能力，以便满足海外买家不断增长的需求。
case/keɪs/T1
考场判断法：① 先看 case 是单独的名词还是固定词块的一部分——in case / in that case / in any case 三块必须整块记，意思分别是「万一」「既然如此」「无论如何」；② 单独的名词 case，看它后面的介词搭配：of murder / of theft → 案件，of flu / of TB → 病例，study / in point → 案例；③ 注意 in case 后面跟完整句子，不能加 the（the case is different）；④ in that case 的 that 是指示代词，in any case 的 any 是泛指，两者不能互换。

情况；事态；情形

in that case 介词短语
In that case, we had better change our plan at once.
那样的话，我们最好立刻改变计划。
in this case 介词短语
In this case, the police found no useful evidence.
在这个案子里，警方没有找到有用的证据。
in most cases 介词短语
In most cases, regular exercise improves sleep quality.
在大多数情况下，规律运动能改善睡眠质量。
the case is that 名词词块
The case is that we have run out of time.
情况是我们已经没有时间了。

案件；诉讼案

a murder case 名词词块
The police are still looking for clues after the case was closed last month.
该案上月结案后，警方仍在寻找线索。
close a case 动词搭配
The police are still looking for clues after the case was closed last month.
该案上月结案后，警方仍在寻找线索。
the case against sb 名词词块
The police are still looking for clues after the case was closed last month.
该案上月结案后，警方仍在寻找线索。
case history 动词搭配
The police are still looking for clues after the case was closed last month.
该案上月结案后，警方仍在寻找线索。

病例；病人个案

a case of flu 名词词块
Doctors said the case was unusual, and that it required careful treatment in hospital.
医生说这个病例不寻常，需要在医院里精心治疗。
a severe case of 名词词块
Doctors said the case was unusual, and that it required careful treatment in hospital.
医生说这个病例不寻常，需要在医院里精心治疗。
a confirmed case 名词词块
Doctors said the case was unusual, and that it required careful treatment in hospital.
医生说这个病例不寻常，需要在医院里精心治疗。
treat the case 动词搭配
Doctors said the case was unusual, and that it required careful treatment in hospital.
医生说这个病例不寻常，需要在医院里精心治疗。

例子；典型案例

a case in point 名词词块
This case is often cited to show how quickly information spreads online.
这个案例常被用来说明信息在网上传播得有多快。
a case study 名词词块
This case is often cited to show how quickly information spreads online.
这个案例常被用来说明信息在网上传播得有多快。
a typical case 名词词块
This case is often cited to show how quickly information spreads online.
这个案例常被用来说明信息在网上传播得有多快。
cite a case 动词搭配
This case is often cited to show how quickly information spreads online.
这个案例常被用来说明信息在网上传播得有多快。

（in case）万一；以防万一

in case 介词短语
Take an umbrella with you in case it rains on the way back.
带把伞，以防回程下雨。
just in case 动词+介词
Take an umbrella with you in case it rains on the way back.
带把伞，以防回程下雨。
in case of 介词短语
Take an umbrella with you in case it rains on the way back.
带把伞，以防回程下雨。
in case it rains 介词短语
Take an umbrella with you in case it rains on the way back.
带把伞，以防回程下雨。
challenge/ˈtʃæləndʒ/T1
考场判断法：① 动词后面接「说法、观点、假设、结论」这类信念类名词 → 必是「质疑、反驳」，不是「挑战难度」；② 动词后面接人并构成 challenge sb to do → 才是「向…挑战」；③ 名词前有 pose / represent / be a challenge to → 是「对…的质疑」；④ 名词单独作宾语讲困难 → 「挑战、难题」。高考里 challenge 当动词几乎都是「质疑」，只背名词义就会整片选错。

挑战、难题（需要克服的困难）

face a challenge 动词搭配
Every new job means you will face a challenge at the start.
每份新工作都意味着一开始就要面对挑战。
a serious challenge 名词词块
Protecting the reef is a serious challenge for scientists.
保护珊瑚礁对科学家是一项严峻挑战。
meet the challenge 动词搭配
The team worked hard to meet the challenge of the deadline.
团队努力应对截止日期的挑战。
an exciting challenge 名词词块
Starting my own business has been an exciting challenge.
自主创业一直是一项令人兴奋的挑战。

（对既有认知的）质疑、挑战

pose a challenge to 动词+介词
Her findings pose a challenge to widely accepted ideas in the field.
她的发现对这一领域中被广泛接受的观念构成了挑战。
a challenge to traditional thinking 名词词块
Her findings pose a challenge to widely accepted ideas in the field.
她的发现对这一领域中被广泛接受的观念构成了挑战。
represent a challenge 动词搭配
Climate change represents a serious challenge to us all.
气候变化对我们所有人构成严峻挑战。

质疑、反驳（对某个说法提出疑问）

challenge the view that 动词搭配
Several researchers challenged the old assumption that talent alone finally decides a person's success.
几位研究者质疑了「仅凭天赋就能决定一个人的成功」这一假设。
challenge sb's claim 动词搭配
Several researchers challenged the old assumption that talent alone finally decides a person's success.
几位研究者质疑了「仅凭天赋就能决定一个人的成功」这一假设。
challenge an assumption 动词搭配
Several researchers challenged the old assumption that talent alone finally decides a person's success.
几位研究者质疑了「仅凭天赋就能决定一个人的成功」这一假设。
be challenged by 系表搭配
Several researchers challenged the old assumption that talent alone finally decides a person's success.
几位研究者质疑了「仅凭天赋就能决定一个人的成功」这一假设。

向…发起挑战、要求对方做

challenge sb to a duel 动词+介词
The young team challenged their rivals to a friendly match on the last day.
这支年轻队在最后一天向对手发起了一场友谊赛的挑战。
challenge sb to do sth 动词+介词
The young team challenged their rivals to a friendly match on the last day.
这支年轻队在最后一天向对手发起了一场友谊赛的挑战。
challenge sb to a match 动词+介词
The young team challenged their rivals to a friendly match on the last day.
这支年轻队在最后一天向对手发起了一场友谊赛的挑战。
characteristic/ˌkærəktəˈrɪstɪk/T1
考场判断法：① 出现 be characteristic of sb/sth → 形容词义「是…的典型特征」，主语一定是被描述的人或物；② 前面有 a key / an obvious 且后面接 of + 对象 → 名词「特征、特点」；③ 形容人的优秀表现 → 「品质、素养」。写作用词时优先用 the key characteristic of…，这是议论文的高分表达。

典型的、特有的（是…的典型特征）

be characteristic of 系表搭配
Long winters are characteristic of this part of the country.
漫长冬季是这个地区的特点。
a characteristic style 名词词块
Her paintings show a characteristic style of bright colours.
她的画展现出以明亮色彩为特色的风格。
characteristic of the period 动词+介词
The tall buildings are characteristic of the period.
这些高楼是那个时代的典型特征。

特征、特点、性质

a key characteristic 名词词块
Curiosity is a key characteristic of a good researcher.
好奇心是优秀研究者的关键特征。
the characteristics of 名词词块
One important characteristic of the language is that it has no written form at all.
这种语言的一个重要特征是它根本没有书面形式。
an obvious characteristic 名词词块
One important characteristic of the language is that it has no written form at all.
这种语言的一个重要特征是它根本没有书面形式。
distinctive characteristics 动词搭配
One important characteristic of the language is that it has no written form at all.
这种语言的一个重要特征是它根本没有书面形式。

品质、素养（人的优良素质）

fine characteristics 动词搭配
Patience is the quality that this young teacher has shown again and again in class.
耐心是这位年轻老师在课堂上一次次展现出的品质。
a noble characteristic 名词词块
Patience is the quality that this young teacher has shown again and again in class.
耐心是这位年轻老师在课堂上一次次展现出的品质。
characteristics of a leader 动词+介词
Patience is the quality that this young teacher has shown again and again in class.
耐心是这位年轻老师在课堂上一次次展现出的品质。
charge/tʃɑ:dʒ/T1
考场判断法：① 先看介词和搭配：in charge of / take charge of → 「掌管、负责」；be charged with + 罪名 → 「指控」；be charged with + 任务 → 「承担、负责」；② charge sb for sth 是「向某人收费」，charge sb with sth 是「指控某人」，for 与 with 不能互换；③ free of charge 是唯一不表示「负责／指控」的搭配，意思是「免费」；④ 看到 charge at / charge into 就是「猛冲」，不是「收费」。

控告、以某罪名指控（某人）

be charged with 系表搭配
He was charged with breaking the law last month.
他上月被指控违法。
charge sb with sth 动词+介词
The police charged the man with theft.
警方指控这个男人犯盗窃罪。
face a charge 动词搭配
The driver now faces a charge of dangerous driving.
这名司机现在面临危险驾驶的指控。
be charged with fraud 系表搭配
The manager was charged with fraud and appeared in court.
经理被控欺诈并出庭受审。

掌管、负责（in charge of）

in charge of 介词短语
She is in charge of the project that will be completed next year.
她负责的项目将于明年完成。
be in charge 系表搭配
She is in charge of the project that will be completed next year.
她负责的项目将于明年完成。
take charge of 动词+介词
She is in charge of the project that will be completed next year.
她负责的项目将于明年完成。
put sb in charge of 动词+介词
She is in charge of the project that will be completed next year.
她负责的项目将于明年完成。

承担、负责（某项任务）

be charged with a task 系表搭配
Each team was charged with solving a problem that had puzzled engineers for years.
每个小组都承担了一项困扰工程师多年的难题。
be charged with doing sth 系表搭配
Each team was charged with solving a problem that had puzzled engineers for years.
每个小组都承担了一项困扰工程师多年的难题。
charge oneself with 动词+介词
Each team was charged with solving a problem that had puzzled engineers for years.
每个小组都承担了一项困扰工程师多年的难题。

猛冲；扑上去

charge at sb 动词+介词
The bull charged at the fence as the crowd stepped back in alarm.
人群惊叫着后退时，那头公牛猛地朝栅栏冲了过来。
charge into 动词+介词
The bull charged at the fence as the crowd stepped back in alarm.
人群惊叫着后退时，那头公牛猛地朝栅栏冲了过来。
charge down 动词搭配
The bull charged at the fence as the crowd stepped back in alarm.
人群惊叫着后退时，那头公牛猛地朝栅栏冲了过来。
charge the goal 动词搭配
The bull charged at the fence as the crowd stepped back in alarm.
人群惊叫着后退时，那头公牛猛地朝栅栏冲了过来。

收费；给…充电

charge a fee 动词搭配
The hotel charges an extra fee for guests who arrive after midnight.
酒店对午夜后到达的客人额外收费。
charge sb for sth 动词+介词
The hotel charges an extra fee for guests who arrive after midnight.
酒店对午夜后到达的客人额外收费。
free of charge 动词+介词
The hotel charges an extra fee for guests who arrive after midnight.
酒店对午夜后到达的客人额外收费。
charge the battery 动词搭配
The hotel charges an extra fee for guests who arrive after midnight.
酒店对午夜后到达的客人额外收费。
concern/kənˈsɜ:n/T1
考场判断法：① 看到 as far as … is concerned 或句首的 concerning，必是「涉及、关系到」，按「就…而言」翻译，与「担心」无关；② 名词前有 a major / a growing 修饰时，指的是「值得关注的事」这一事物，不是情绪；③ 后面接人（concern sb）或出现 with concern，才落到「担忧」的感情色彩上。

涉及、关系到（与某事有关）

as far as sb is concerned 动词搭配
As far as I am concerned, the plan is worth trying.
就我而言，这个计划值得一试。
concerning 动词搭配
She asked a question concerning the cost of the trip.
她问了一个关于旅行费用的问题。
be concerned with 系表搭配
This section is concerned with the history of the school.
这一部分涉及学校的历史。
concern oneself with 动词+介词
Do not concern yourself with details that do not matter.
不要为无关紧要的细节操心。

关切之事、值得关注的问题

a growing concern 名词词块
Safety on the road has become a growing concern for families with young children.
道路安全已日益成为家有幼儿的家庭关注的问题。
a major concern 名词词块
Rising costs are a major concern for many families.
物价上涨是许多家庭的主要担忧。
a public concern 名词词块
Safety on the road has become a growing concern for families with young children.
道路安全已日益成为家有幼儿的家庭关注的问题。
of great concern 介词短语
Safety on the road has become a growing concern for families with young children.
道路安全已日益成为家有幼儿的家庭关注的问题。

使…感到担忧、挂念

concern sb 动词搭配
The rising cost of housing concerns many young families who move to big cities.
不断上涨的住房成本让许多迁往大城市的年轻家庭感到担忧。
be concerned about 系表搭配
The rising cost of housing concerns many young families who move to big cities.
不断上涨的住房成本让许多迁往大城市的年轻家庭感到担忧。
concerned about sth 动词+介词
The rising cost of housing concerns many young families who move to big cities.
不断上涨的住房成本让许多迁往大城市的年轻家庭感到担忧。

忧虑、担心的情绪

with concern 介词短语
She looked at the old photograph with obvious concern, as if she had lost something precious.
她带着明显的忧虑看着那张旧照片，仿佛失去了什么珍贵的东西。
out of concern for 动词+介词
She looked at the old photograph with obvious concern, as if she had lost something precious.
她带着明显的忧虑看着那张旧照片，仿佛失去了什么珍贵的东西。
show concern 动词搭配
She looked at the old photograph with obvious concern, as if she had lost something precious.
她带着明显的忧虑看着那张旧照片，仿佛失去了什么珍贵的东西。
deep concern 动词搭配
She looked at the old photograph with obvious concern, as if she had lost something precious.
她带着明显的忧虑看着那张旧照片，仿佛失去了什么珍贵的东西。
conduct/kənˈdʌkt/T1
考场判断法：① 先定词性：出现在主语位置（the team conducts a survey）→ 动词义；出现在名词位置（his conduct was acceptable）→ 「行为、举止」义；② 动词后面接 survey / research / experiment / study / interview 这类「调查类名词」→ 「进行、实施」；接 a meeting / a ceremony / a competition → 「组织、筹办」；接 an orchestra / a choir → 「指挥」；③ conduct a survey 与 carry out a survey 意思相同，但高考语料更常用 conduct；④ conduct oneself「表现、举止」是固定搭配，只能整块记。

行为方式、举止；操行

good conduct 动词搭配
The student was praised for her good conduct in class.
这名女生因课堂表现良好受到表扬。
conduct unbecoming 动词搭配
The officer was warned for conduct unbecoming a leader.
这名军官因有失身份的行为受到警告。
a model of conduct 名词词块
His calm behaviour was a model of conduct for us all.
他冷静的举止是我们所有人的行为楷模。

进行、实施（调查与实验）

conduct a survey 动词搭配
The researchers conducted a survey among 500 students.
研究人员在500名学生中做了一项调查。
conduct research 动词搭配
The research team conducted a survey among students from different schools last term.
该研究团队上学期在来自不同学校的学生中开展了一项调查。
conduct an experiment 动词搭配
The research team conducted a survey among students from different schools last term.
该研究团队上学期在来自不同学校的学生中开展了一项调查。
conduct an interview 动词搭配
The research team conducted a survey among students from different schools last term.
该研究团队上学期在来自不同学校的学生中开展了一项调查。

组织、筹办（活动与比赛）

conduct a meeting 动词搭配
The school will conduct a sports meeting next month, to which all students are welcome.
学校将于下月举办运动会，欢迎所有学生参加。
conduct a ceremony 动词搭配
The school will conduct a sports meeting next month, to which all students are welcome.
学校将于下月举办运动会，欢迎所有学生参加。
conduct a competition 动词搭配
The school will conduct a sports meeting next month, to which all students are welcome.
学校将于下月举办运动会，欢迎所有学生参加。

指挥、带领（乐队或队伍）

conduct an orchestra 动词搭配
She conducted the orchestra without a score, surprising everyone in the hall.
她不看谱指挥了整支乐团，让全场的人都吃了一惊。

consequence/ˈkɒnsɪkwəns/T1
考场判断法：① 前面有 the / its 且后面接 of + 从句 → 「结果、后果」；② 句中出现 of little / no / great consequence → 是「重要性、紧要性」，绝不译成「后果」；③ 看到 in consequence 单独作状语 → 相当于 therefore，是逻辑连接词，按「因此」读，不要当成名词。

结果、结局（某种行为带来的）

the consequences of doing sth 名词词块
He understood the consequences of telling a lie.
他明白说谎的后果。

Careless driving can have serious consequences.
粗心驾驶可能造成严重后果。
a consequence of 名词词块
The fault was a consequence of poor design.
这个缺陷是设计不佳造成的。
unintended consequences 动词搭配
The new rule had several unintended consequences.
这项新规定带来了几个意料之外的后果。

重要性、紧要性

of little consequence 介词短语
Whether the meeting goes ahead is of little consequence to the rest of the plan.
会议是否照常举行，对计划的其他部分无关紧要。
of no consequence 介词短语
Whether the meeting goes ahead is of little consequence to the rest of the plan.
会议是否照常举行，对计划的其他部分无关紧要。
of great consequence 介词短语
Whether the meeting goes ahead is of little consequence to the rest of the plan.
会议是否照常举行，对计划的其他部分无关紧要。
of much consequence 介词短语
Whether the meeting goes ahead is of little consequence to the rest of the plan.
会议是否照常举行，对计划的其他部分无关紧要。

The road was blocked for repairs; in consequence, several classes were delayed that afternoon.

content/ˈkɒntent/ · /kənˈtent/T1
考场判断法：① 先看它前面有没有 be：be content with / to do 是形容词「满足的」，而名词 content 不与 be 连用；② the content of a message 是「内容」，the contents of a book 永远带 s，是「目录」；③ 食品、健康类语境里出现 high / low + content，一律读作「含量」。另记词形链 content（内容）→ contented（满意的）→ contentment（满足）。

He was content to stay at home and read all day.
他满足于整天待在家里看书。

They contented themselves with a short walk after dinner.
他们满足于饭后散步一小会儿。

contrast/ˈkɒntrɑ:st/T1
考场判断法：① 先圈介词：by contrast（相比之下，后面常跟完整句子）vs in contrast to / in contrast with（与…形成对比，后面必须接比较对象），两组必须整块记；② 动词 contrast A with B 是「把 A 与 B 作对比」；③ 若 with 后面接的是预期、说法这类内容（contrast with expectations），就是「与…截然不同」；④ 不要把 contrast（对比）与 compare（比较）、compare with（与…相比）混为一谈。

对比、对照；差异

in contrast 介词短语
In contrast, life in the city is much faster.
相比之下，城市生活节奏快得多。

作者把城市生活与小村庄的生活作了对比。

critical/ˈkrɪtɪkl/T1
考场判断法：① 先看介词：be critical of → 「对…持批评态度」；be critical to / for → 「对…至关重要」；② 看到 critical + condition / moment / stage / situation → 「危急的、危重的」，不是「批判的」；③ 看到 critical acclaim 或 critical reviews → 「评论界的（好评）」；④ 注意 be critical to do 与 be critical for sth 的区别：前者是「必须做某事」，后者是「对某物重要」。

评论家对这部新电影批评激烈。

批判态度帮助学生质疑观点。

她对这个方案发表了批评意见。

至关重要的、决定性的（be critical to）

清洁的水对该地区每个社区的健康都至关重要。

清洁的水对该地区每个社区的健康都至关重要。

develop/dɪˈveləp/T1
考场判断法：① develop 后面接 habit / interest / reputation，是「养成、发展出」；② 接 cold / disease / symptoms，是「患上」；③ 接 film / photograph，是「冲洗」；④ 接 into（develop into a serious problem），是「发展成、变成」；⑤ 宾语是城市、技术、经济这类，才是「发展、开发」。语法填空常考现在分词作状语（Developing rapidly, the company…），按「正在发展的」理解。

The region plans to develop its natural resources carefully.
该地区计划谨慎开发自然资源。

effect/ɪˈfekt/T1
考场判断法：① 先定词性再加搭配：have an effect on = 有影响；take effect / come into effect = 生效；side effects = 副作用；in effect = 实际上；② affect 是动词「影响」，effect 是名词「影响」，看到 have a great ___ on，空格后是 on，必须填名词 effect；③ 作动词的 effect（to effect a change）只出现在正式语体，表示「促成、实现」，不要与 affect 混用；④ 读句子时先找 effect 前面有没有 a / the / this 这类限定词，有就是名词义。

影响、作用（have an effect on）

have an effect on 动词+介词
Exercise has a positive effect on both body and mind.
运动对身心都有积极影响。
have a great effect on 动词+介词
Early education has a great effect on a child’s future.
早期教育对孩子未来影响很大。
an effect on sth 名词词块
The new rule had an immediate effect on attendance.
新规定立刻对出勤率产生了影响。
no effect 动词搭配
The medicine had no effect on his headache.
这种药对他的头痛没有效果。

新校规将于下周一生效。

The medicine had a quick effect on her headache and she slept well that night.
这种药对她的头痛很快就见效了，那晚她睡得很好。

The medicine had a quick effect on her headache and she slept well that night.

emphasis/ˈemfəsɪs/T1
考场判断法：① 出现 place / put / lay emphasis on sb/sth → 一律译成「强调、重视」，这是应用文写作的固定词块；② 上下文谈的是句子重音、发音着力点 → 「重音、重读」；③ 出现 a shift in emphasis / the emphasis of… → 「重点、侧重之处」。注意 emphasis（名词，强调）与 emphatic（形容词，强调的）、empirical（经验的）常被混选。

单词重音能改变句子的意思。

学校重视实践技能。

flat/flæt/T1
考场判断法：① 修饰 story / voice / atmosphere / joke → 形容词「平淡乏味的」，这是完形里 flat 的主要用法；② 修饰 plain / surface / land → 「平坦的、水平的」；③ 前面有 a / the 并指住处（a studio flat）→ 名词「公寓」；④ 修饰 rate / fee → 固定搭配「划一的」。flat 全靠后面接的名词分辨。

former/ˈfɔ:mə(r)/T1
考场判断法：① 句中出现 the former 一定与 the latter 成对使用，译成「前者 / 后者」，绝不译成「以前的」——这是七选五与完形的高频坑；② 修饰人（former workers / former champion）才是「以前的、先前的」；③ 前面有 the 且指「创办人」（the formers of the movement）→ 名词义「创始人」，这是它最冷僻的一层。

the former ... the latter 名词词块

gap/gæp/T1
考场判断法：① the gap between A and B → 一定是「差距」，也是作文里 bridge the gap 的固定对象；② a gap in + 具体事物（wall / market / record / knowledge）→ 「缺口、豁口或空白」；③ 后面跟时间（a gap of two years / a gap between two lectures）→ 「间隔、空档」。

理论与实践之间有很大差距。
narrow the gap 动词搭配
Extra classes help narrow the gap between students.
补习课有助于缩小学生间的差距。

impact/ˈɪmpækt/T1
考场判断法：① impact 后面跟 on，或前面出现 have / make，一律先读成「影响」，再看词性；② 只有句子在讲物体相撞相砸（car、storm、meteorite、debris），才读「撞击、冲击」；③ 名词用 a huge impact on sth，动词用 impact sth，中间不加介词 on，介词误加是语法填空高频错点。

input/ˈɪnpʊt/T1
考场判断法：① 后面是 into / a system / data / a password → 动词「输入、键入」；② 后面跟 of + money / time / energy → 「投入」；③ 语境里出现 advice / comment / suggestion，说的是「别人的意见」→ 「供参考的消息、线索」；④ 名词指送进系统的东西才是「输入的内容、数据」。input 与 output 是一组必背对比，impute（把…归因于）是形近误选重灾区。

她的建议为我们最终提交的方案提供了宝贵的参考。
interest/ˈɪntrəst/T1
考场判断法：① 句中出现 rate / rates / loan / bank / deposit → 直接锁定「利息」；② 出现复数 interests 或 in the interest of → 「利益、权益」；③ interested 作形容词时，只有 be interested in、be interested to do 才是「对…感兴趣」，an interested party 是「利益相关方」；④ 动词 interest 后面直接跟人、不接 in，就是「引起…的兴趣」。

兴趣、爱好（对某事的喜爱）

have an interest in 动词+介词
She has a strong interest in ancient history.
她对古代史有浓厚兴趣。
show interest in 动词+介词
The child showed interest in the new toy.
这个孩子对新玩具表现出兴趣。
a wide range of interests 名词词块
He has a wide range of interests, from music to sport.
他兴趣广泛，从音乐到运动都有。
with great interest 介词短语
The students listened to the story with great interest.
学生们兴致勃勃地听这个故事。

利息（借钱或存款所付的钱）

随着利率上升，许多家庭正在重新考虑储蓄计划。

high interest 动词搭配

利益、好处（多为复数）

这个决定是出于安全而非盈利的考虑。
conflicting interests 动词搭配

这个决定是出于安全而非盈利的考虑。
a vested interest 名词词块

这个决定是出于安全而非盈利的考虑。
serve the interests of 动词+介词
The decision was made in the interest of safety rather than profit.
这个决定是出于安全而非盈利的考虑。

使感兴趣、引起关注

一个普通农民的故事一定会引起文学专业学生的兴趣。

一个普通农民的故事一定会引起文学专业学生的兴趣。

一个普通农民的故事一定会引起文学专业学生的兴趣。

有关系的、利害相关的（利益方）

an interested party 名词词块
All interested parties, including local residents and traders, were invited to comment.
所有利益相关方，包括当地居民和商家，都受邀提出意见。

major/ˈmeɪdʒə(r)/T1
考场判断法：① major 后面接 part / share / portion 这类表示「份额」的词，译成「大部分」，不是「主要的」；② 接 factor / concern / role / change 这类抽象名词才是「主要的、重大的」；③ 前面有 my / his / her 且指学业方向 → 名词「主修专业」；④ 后面带 in + 学科名 → 动词「主修」。major 与 minor 是一组必背的形近对比。

主要的、重大的（占主导的）

Rising costs are a major concern for many families.

a major factor 名词词块
Cost is a major factor in choosing a university.
费用是选择大学的一个主要因素。
a major role 名词词块
Parents play a major role in a child’s education.
父母在孩子教育中起主要作用。
a major change 名词词块
There was a major change in the school timetable.
课程表有了重大变化。

the major portion of 名词词块

这项工作的绝大部分是由当地社区的志愿者完成的。

mean/miːn/T1
考场判断法：① 后面跟 to do（mean to do）→ 「打算」，mean to say 同理；② 出现在句末作结论（That means... / It means a lot to me）→ 「意味着」；③ 前面有 the，后面跟 score / age / price → 是名词「平均数」；④ 后面跟 money，或构成 too mean to do → 形容词「吝啬的」；⑤ mean 是同形三义词（动词、名词、形容词），语法填空先定词性再定义项：mean value、mean temperature 是形容词「平均的」，the mean 是名词「平均数」。

意味着、表示（某符号或事实所代表的）

我不是有意伤害你的感情。
what does it mean 动词搭配
What does this sign mean on the map?
地图上这个符号是什么意思？
mean much to sb 动词+介词
This old photo means a great deal to my grandmother.
这张老照片对我奶奶意义重大。
it means that 动词搭配
A red light means that you must stop.
红灯意味着你必须停车。

measure/ˈmeʒə(r)/T1
考场判断法：① 先看单复数：measures（复数）+ to do sth → 「措施、办法」，这是高考最常考的搭配；② 看到 a measure of，后面是 length / weight / height / temperature → 「尺寸、量度」，是 success / ability / progress → 「衡量…的尺度」；③ 动词 measure 接 impact / effect / performance → 「衡量、评估」，不是「测量尺寸」；④ 注意 take measures to do sth 是应用文写作的固定搭配，后接动词原形，不要写成 take measures to doing。

The app can measure the distance you run.
这个应用能测量你跑步的距离。

He used a tape measure to check the window.
他用卷尺量了窗户。

mind/maɪnd/T1
考场判断法：① 看到 Do you mind / Would you mind doing 必是「介意」，后面若接 if 从句，从句要用陈述语序（Do you mind if I open the window?）；② keep sth in mind 与 bear in mind 同义，是「记在心上」，不是「介意」；③ mind your head / mind your step 是「当心」；④ 名词 mind 后接 change（change your mind）、put（put your mind to）时是「想法、心思」。mind doing 与 mind to do 的介词选择是应用文高频错点。

minor/ˈmaɪnə(r)/T1
考场判断法：① 先看修饰的对象：修饰 problem / role / detail / issue → 「次要的、不重要的」；修饰 injury / accident / damage → 「轻微的」；② 看到 the minor 或 minors 作名词 → 「未成年人」，这是法律义，跟大小无关；③ major in 是主修，minor in 是辅修、兼修，两个词只差一个字母，含义相反，看到 ___ in economics 要先判断说的是谁；④ minor 与 major、minimum、minute 构成形近对比组，必须打包记。

不重要的、无关紧要的

a minor role 名词词块
He played a minor role in the school play.
他在校园剧中扮演一个小角色。

This is only a minor problem, do not worry.
这只是个小问题，别担心。
a minor detail 名词词块
Do not spend time on every minor detail.
不要在每一个小细节上花时间。
a minor issue 名词词块
We can deal with that as a minor issue later.
那个可以作为小问题稍后处理。

程度较轻的（伤势、事故）

nature/ˈneɪtʃə/T1
考场判断法：① 主语是 human beings / children，或者 it is human nature to do → 锁定「天性、本性」；② 前面出现 the，接 the nature of the problem、the nature of the work → 是「性质、本质」；③ 出现 in nature 单独作状语（in nature a form of ...）→ 是「本质上」；④ 其余讲山川、树林、环保的才是「自然、大自然」。

nerve/nɜ:v/T1
考场判断法：① 语境里出现 body / brain / muscle / pain / damaged → 神经义；② 出现 have the nerve to do、lose one's nerve、pluck up one's nerve → 勇气义，一律搭配 one's nerve 或 to do；③ get on sb's nerves 是「让某人烦躁」，不是「神经上的」；④ nerve 单数多指神经或勇气，nerves 复数多指情绪上的紧张。

a damaged nerve 名词词块
A damaged nerve can cause pain or numbness.
受损的神经会引起疼痛或麻木。

order/ˈɔːdə/T1
考场判断法：① 看到 in order to do，立刻锁定「为了」，别当成「按…顺序」；② 动词后面跟人并带 to do 或 about → 「命令、指示」；后面跟商品、机票、书 → 「订购」；③ 名词前有 keep / maintain / restore → 「秩序」，这是社会类文章的高频词；④ 名词接 the order of → 「顺序」。order 的名词义（命令、秩序、顺序）与动词义（命令、订购）都要分清，order sb to do 和 order a ticket 意思完全不同。

顺序、次序（事物排列的先后）

in order of 介词短语

学生们按身高排队。

Put these steps in the right order.
把这些步骤按正确顺序排列。
a fixed order 名词词块
The seats are arranged in a fixed order.
座位按固定顺序排列。

Great care is taken to keep order and safety at all public events.
所有公共活动都十分注意维持秩序与安全。

所有公共活动都十分注意维持秩序与安全。

Great care is taken to keep order and safety at all public events.
所有公共活动都十分注意维持秩序与安全。

particular/pəˈtɪkjələ(r)/T1
考场判断法：① 先圈搭配：be particular about sth → 「对…讲究、挑剔」；in particular → 「尤其、特别是」；particulars（复数名词）→ 「详情、细项」；② 看到 in this particular case / on that particular day → 「特定的、具体的」；③ be particular about 后接名词或动名词（about being on time），不接动词不定式；④ particular 的核心义是「特定的」，但高考最爱考的是「挑剔的」和「in particular」，这两块必须整块背。

专指某事的、具体而明确的

in this particular case 介词短语
In this particular case, the rule does not apply.
在这个具体案例中，这条规则不适用。
on that particular day 介词短语
On that particular day, it rained without stopping.
就在那一天，雨下个不停。
a particular example 名词词块
Let me give you a particular example to explain.
让我举一个具体的例子来说明。
particular attention 动词搭配
The teacher paid particular attention to pronunciation.
老师特别注意发音。

过分挑剔的、对细节要求苛刻的（be particular about）

她对吃的食物非常讲究。
particular about food 动词+介词

她对吃的东西很讲究，周末也很少在外面吃饭。
too particular to please 动词+介词
She is very particular about her food and rarely eats outside on weekends.
她对吃的东西很讲究，周末也很少在外面吃饭。

医生在第一周特别关注患者的睡眠情况。

医生在第一周特别关注患者的睡眠情况。

pattern/ˈpætn/T1
考场判断法：① 先判词性——后面直接带宾语并出现 after / on，多半是动词「仿制」义（pattern sth after sb）；② 句子里出现 behaviour / decline / trend / development 这类社会词，是名词「模式、规律」义；③ 只有提到布料、墙纸、桌布、器皿表面花纹时才读「图案」；④ 讲某人的做法值得别人效仿时，是「典范、榜样」。注意 pattern 在高考里作动词「仿照」是高频坑，语法填空见 pattern 后接名词就先想动词义。

图案、花样（重复出现的图形组合）

a repeating pattern 名词词块
The wallpaper has a repeating pattern of stars.
壁纸上有星星重复的图案。
a floral pattern 名词词块
She chose a dress with a floral pattern.
她选了一条有花卉图案的连衣裙。
a geometric pattern 名词词块
The floor was laid in a geometric pattern.
地板按几何图案铺设。
a pattern on the fabric 名词词块
There is a small pattern on the fabric.
这块布料上有小图案。

言语刻薄成了他的一种行为模式。
a pattern of decline 名词词块
Studies suggest that a pattern of short sleep may lead to lower concentration at school.
研究显示，睡眠时间偏少的规律可能导致上课时注意力下降。
a growing pattern 名词词块
Studies suggest that a pattern of short sleep may lead to lower concentration at school.
研究显示，睡眠时间偏少的规律可能导致上课时注意力下降。
follow a pattern 动词搭配
The story follows a pattern familiar to many readers.
这个故事遵循许多读者熟悉的套路。

仿制、照…的样子做

the ideal pattern of 名词词块
Her patience with beginners set a pattern of service that many volunteers now follow.
她对初学者的耐心树立了服务典范，许多志愿者如今都效仿她。
phase/feɪz/T1
考场判断法：① 看到 in phases / phase by phase，固定读成「逐步、分阶段地」，整块背下来；② phase out 是「逐步淘汰」，别拆成「淘汰一个阶段」；③ 只有语境里出现 wave / signal / current 这些物理词，才可能读成「相位」；④ 其余一律是「阶段、时期」，搭配记 a phase of 和 in this phase。

阶段、时期（事物发展的一个段落）

a phase of 名词词块
The project is in the first phase of its development.
项目处于发展的第一阶段。

In this phase, students focus on basic skills.
在这一阶段，学生专注于基本技能。
the final phase 名词词块
The final phase of the plan starts in October.
计划的最后阶段从十月开始。
a new phase 名词词块
The project has entered a new phase of development.
项目进入了新的发展阶段。

相位（波形、信号、电波的位相）

the phase of the wave 名词词块
Two sound waves with the same frequency but different phase behave quite differently.
频率相同但相位不同的两列声波表现相当不同。
in phase 介词短语
Two sound waves with the same frequency but different phase behave quite differently.
频率相同但相位不同的两列声波表现相当不同。
out of phase 动词+介词
Two sound waves with the same frequency but different phase behave quite differently.
频率相同但相位不同的两列声波表现相当不同。
phase difference 动词搭配
Two sound waves with the same frequency but different phase behave quite differently.
频率相同但相位不同的两列声波表现相当不同。

The cleanup of the river will be carried out in phases to reduce traffic trouble.
河道清理将分阶段进行，以减少交通干扰。

The cleanup of the river will be carried out in phases to reduce traffic trouble.
河道清理将分阶段进行，以减少交通干扰。

The cleanup of the river will be carried out in phases to reduce traffic trouble.
河道清理将分阶段进行，以减少交通干扰。
in three phases 介词短语
The cleanup of the river will be carried out in phases to reduce traffic trouble.
河道清理将分阶段进行，以减少交通干扰。

逐步淘汰、逐步停用

phase out 动词搭配
The factory plans to phase out the old machines.
工厂计划逐步淘汰旧机器。
phase in 动词+介词
The school will phase in the new timetable over a term.
学校将在一个学期内逐步推行新时间表。
be phased out 系表搭配
The city plans to phase out the oldest diesel buses within three years.
该市计划在三年内逐步淘汰最老旧的柴油公交车。
phase out gradually 动词搭配
The city plans to phase out the oldest diesel buses within three years.
该市计划在三年内逐步淘汰最老旧的柴油公交车。
plant/plɑːnt/T1
考场判断法：① 看 plant 前面有没有 power / chemical / steel / nuclear 这类行业修饰语，有就直接锁定「工厂、发电站」；② 看 plant 后面接的是 seeds / crops / trees（活的东西）还是 suspicion / an idea / a flag（想法、猜疑），前者是「种植」，后者是「安放、植入」；③ 话题涉及 pollution / emission / environment 时，plant 只能读成「工厂」，不能读成「植物」。

植物；草木等生物体

flowering plant 动词搭配
A rose is a common flowering plant.
玫瑰是一种常见的开花植物。

The park is home to many rare plant species.
这个公园是许多珍稀植物物种的家园。

point/pɔɪnt/T1
考场判断法：① point 后面接 of view / of mine / a valid one，是「观点、看法」，不要译成「点」；② point 后面接 to（point to the evidence / point to a decline）是动词「指向、表明」，表示具体方位用 at，表示抽象趋势用 to；③ 出现 the point of doing / miss the point / make the point，一律按「要点、用意」理解；④ 写作里表达「观点」固定写 point of view，不要写 viewpoint of。

点、细部；（时间或空间上的）一点

a key point 名词词块

继续之前我说明一个关键点。

At this point, no one knew the answer.
此时没有人知道答案。
a turning point 名词词块
Winning the match was a turning point in his career.
赢得那场比赛是他职业生涯的转折点。
point by point 动词+介词
She explained her plan point by point.
她逐点解释了自己的计划。

最新数据显示，吸烟的年轻人数量在稳步下降。

Recent data points to a steady decline in the number of young people who smoke.
最新数据显示，吸烟的年轻人数量在稳步下降。

老师指出了我答案中的错误。
point to a decline 动词+介词
Recent data points to a steady decline in the number of young people who smoke.
最新数据显示，吸烟的年轻人数量在稳步下降。

position/pəˈzɪʃn/T1
考场判断法：① 先看前面的动词：hold / apply for / offer a position → 「职位」；take a position on → 「立场、看法」；② 看到 in a position to do sth → 「有能力、有条件做某事」，后面必须跟动词不定式；③ 表示「在…位置上」时用介词 in 或 at（in a position / at the centre），不要用动词 position in；④ position 作动词时常见的是被动分词 positioned，这是语法填空的高频形态。

power/ˈpaʊə(r)/T1
考场判断法：① 先判断语境属哪一类：政治与国际话题 → 「权力、势力」；能源与自然话题 → 「电力、能量」；个人能力话题 → the power to do sth = 「做某事的能力」；② 看到 electric power / power supply / power cut 一律是电力义；③ power 表「力量」时是不可数名词，但 a power station、powers 是别的词；④ 高考常把 power 与 right 放在一起考：power 是「能力、权力」，right 是「权利、正确」，方向相反不要互换。

她在辩论中很有说服力。

practice/ˈpræktɪs/T1
考场判断法：① practice 前面有 common / standard / usual，是「惯例、做法」，不是「练习」；② in practice 意为「实际上」（= in reality），不是「在练习中」；③ 后面接 doing（practice speaking English），是动词「练习」；④ medical / legal 语境（medical practice, in clinical practice）是「业务、行医执业」；⑤ 应用文表达「这是一种普遍做法」固定写 It is common practice to do sth，不要写成 common practice of doing。

present/ˈpreznt/T1
考场判断法：① present 后面接 problem / difficulty / challenge，是动词「呈现、带来（问题）」，问题是自己冒出来的；② present sth to sb，to 后面是人或会议，意为「把…呈递、提出」；③ be present at the meeting，at 后接活动场合，是「出席」，此时不能读成「目前的」；④ at present / the present situation 才是「目前」。语法填空中 Present with… 是「目前有」，present 分词作状语（Present in…, the app…）按「目前的、当前的」理解。

principle/ˈprɪnsəpl/T1
考场判断法：① 先看领域：科学、技术、物理语境 → 「原理、法则」；道德、人格、诚信语境 → 「道德准则」；学校、制度、规则语境 → 「原则、准则」；② 注意两组介词必须整块记：in principle = 原则上，on principle = 凭原则做事，the principle of = …的原则；③ on the principle that 后面接整句，是「以…为原则」；④ principle（原则）≠ principal（主要的／校长），后者拼写多一个字母，含义完全不同，属于 E3 形近误判高发点。

原则、准则（做事的依据）

basic principles 动词搭配
The course teaches the basic principles of physics.
这门课讲授物理学的基本原理。

represent/ˌreprɪˈzent/T1
考场判断法：① 先看主语是人、组织还是物：主语是人或组织（two students were chosen to represent…）→ 「代表」；主语是物或抽象概念（the lotus…）→ 「象征、体现」；② 后面跟数字或数量（represent half of the cost）→ 「相当于、占」；③ 出现 in the form of 或 in the painting 时是「以…形式呈现」；④ 注意 representative 是形容词或名词（代表、代表人物），represent 才是动词，别把 representative of 当成 represent。

学校由两名学生代表参加比赛。

This group represents the majority of students.
这个群体代表大多数学生。

result/rɪˈzʌlt/T1
考场判断法：① 只看介词：result in + 名词或 doing 是「导致」，result from + 名词是「源于」，方向不能反；② as a result of + 名词是「由于」，后面接原因；as a result（不接 of）是「因此」，后面接结果；③ 记口诀：from 后面是源头，in 后面是去处。完形填空最常考的就是把 result in 与 result from 读反。

结果、结局；（实验的）成果

the result of 名词词块
The result of the experiment surprised the scientists.
实验结果让科学家感到惊讶。
as a result 动词搭配
He forgot his homework; as a result, he had to stay after class.
他忘了作业，结果放学后被留下。
a satisfactory result 名词词块
He was pleased with a satisfactory result in the exam.
他对考试中令人满意的成绩感到高兴。
come up with a result 动词+介词
The team came up with a result after weeks of work.
团队经过数周工作得出了结果。

导致、造成（引起某种后果）

result in 动词+介词
Careless mistakes resulted in his failure in the exam.
粗心的错误导致他考试失败。
result in doing sth 动词+介词
Careless driving on wet roads often results in serious accidents that could have been avoided.
在湿滑路面上粗心驾驶常常导致本可避免的严重事故。
result in serious damage 动词+介词
Careless driving on wet roads often results in serious accidents that could have been avoided.
在湿滑路面上粗心驾驶常常导致本可避免的严重事故。

Careless driving on wet roads often results in serious accidents that could have been avoided.
在湿滑路面上粗心驾驶常常导致本可避免的严重事故。

源于、起因于（由…而发生）

result from 动词+介词
Her illness resulted from years of overwork.
她的病是多年过度劳累造成的。
result from doing sth 动词+介词
Much of the anxiety students feel before an exam results from constant comparison with classmates.
学生考前的大部分焦虑源于不断与同学攀比。
largely result from 动词+介词
Much of the anxiety students feel before an exam results from constant comparison with classmates.
学生考前的大部分焦虑源于不断与同学攀比。
which results from 动词+介词
Much of the anxiety students feel before an exam results from constant comparison with classmates.
学生考前的大部分焦虑源于不断与同学攀比。

因此、由此；（as a result of）由于

as a result 动词搭配
He forgot his homework; as a result, he had to stay after class.
他忘了作业，结果放学后被留下。
as a result of 动词+介词
As a result of the heavy rain, several village roads were closed for two days.
由于大雨，村里几条道路封闭了两天。
as a direct result 动词搭配
As a result of the heavy rain, several village roads were closed for two days.
由于大雨，村里几条道路封闭了两天。
with the result that 介词短语
It rained hard, with the result that the match was cancelled.
雨下得很大，结果比赛被取消。
right/raɪt/T1
考场判断法：① the right to do sth、human rights 中的 right 是名词「权利」，不是「右边、正确」；② right 后直接跟名词（the right way / the right person / the right choice）是形容词「恰当的」，比 correct 更强调「合适」；③ be right / feel right / look right 才是「正确的」；④ right now / right away / right across from 是副词「正好、立刻」。应用文表达「有权做某事」固定写 the right to do sth。

无论对错，他总是支持朋友。

seek/si:k/T1
考场判断法：① 后面接不定式（seek to do）立刻锁定「试图、力图」，别当成「想要做」；② 后面接 help / advice / permission / support 这类「可获得的资源」是「寻求、设法得到」；③ 后面接 shelter / refuge / treatment / job 这类「要找到的东西」是「寻找、觅得」；④ 后面接 success / happiness / fame 这类「长期想要的状态」是「追求」。seek 是书面语，语法填空里 seek to do 的不定式漏掉 to 就整空错。

寻求、设法得到（帮助、许可、信息）

seek help 动词搭配
Do not be afraid to seek help when you are in trouble.
遇到困难时不要害怕求助。
seek advice 动词搭配
It is wise to seek advice from a teacher before you decide.
决定前征求老师意见是明智的。
seek permission 动词搭配
You must seek permission before using the lab.
使用实验室前必须申请许可。
seek support 动词搭配
The charity sought support from local businesses.
这家慈善机构寻求当地企业的支持。

seek to do sth 动词+介词

许多学生试图通过写英文日记来提高写作水平。

Many students seek to improve their writing by keeping a short diary in English.
许多学生试图通过写英文日记来提高写作水平。
seek to change 动词+介词
Many students seek to improve their writing by keeping a short diary in English.
许多学生试图通过写英文日记来提高写作水平。
seek to find out 动词+介词
Many students seek to improve their writing by keeping a short diary in English.
许多学生试图通过写英文日记来提高写作水平。

After years of hard work, he finally achieved the career he had sought after.

sense/sens/T1
考场判断法：① a + 抽象名词（belonging / responsibility / achievement / purpose）构成 a sense of…，是「意识、观念」，不是「感觉」也不是「意义」；② in this / in a sense 是「意义、含义」；③ make sense 后面不接宾语，是「讲得通、有道理」；make sense of + 宾语是「理解、弄懂」，两个短语必须整块背；④ a sixth sense / sense of smell 才是「感官」义。语法填空最爱考 make sense of。

感觉、感官；官能

sense of smell 动词+介词
Dogs have a very strong sense of smell.
狗的嗅觉非常灵敏。
sense of touch 动词+介词
Blind people rely on their sense of touch to read.
盲人依靠触觉阅读。
a sixth sense 名词词块
She seemed to have a sixth sense for trouble.
她似乎对麻烦有第六感。
common sense 动词搭配
It is only common sense to lock the door at night.
晚上锁门只是常识。

意义、含义；词义

in this sense 介词短语
The word is used here in a much broader sense than its dictionary entry suggests.
这里这个词的用法比词典释义所暗示的要宽泛得多。
in a sense 介词短语
In a sense, the failure was a blessing.
从某种意义上说，这次失败是件好事。
in the sense that 介词短语
The word is used here in a much broader sense than its dictionary entry suggests.
这里这个词的用法比词典释义所暗示的要宽泛得多。

setting/ˈsetɪŋ/T1
考场判断法：① 先看介词：in the setting of a novel = 在…的故事背景下；in a professional / public setting = 在某种场合；② 上下文出现 device / phone / app / TV / camera + setting → 「设定、参数」，这是科技话题词；③ 出现 the setting of the sun 或 the setting sun → 「日落、落下」，是写景类文章的固定表达；④ setting 是 set（设置、放置）的名词形式，读后续写里先想「被设置的状态」，再按语境细分。

shift/ʃɪft/T1
考场判断法：① 出现 a shift in / a shift towards → 名词「转变、转移」，这是社会类文章的核心表达；② 宾语是实物（machine / furniture）→ 动词「搬动、挪动」；③ 出现 shift from A to B → 动词「转向、改换」；④ 跟 night / day / work 连用 → 名词「轮班、班次」。

sound/saʊnd/T1
考场判断法：① sound 后直接跟名词（advice / argument / reasoning / decision / judgment），是形容词「可靠的、站得住脚的」；② sound health / sound condition 是「健全的」；③ sound + 形容词或 sound like，是动词「听起来」；④ sound asleep 是副词「完全地、充分地」，不是「声音」。写应用文表达「睡得很沉」只能写 sound asleep。

spot/spɒt/T1
考场判断法：① 后面接的是 problem / mistake / difference 这类「问题类」名词 → 动词「发现、察觉」；② 后面接的是人（sb / a friend）→ 「认出、辨出」；③ 前面有 a / the 并指一处风景 → 名词「地点、场所」；④ 形容墙、皮肤上的印迹 → 名词「斑点、污点」。spot 作动词是完形里的常客。

stand/stænd/T1
考场判断法：① stand + for：后面接符号、缩写、机构，是「代表、象征」（stand for the Olympic Games）；后面接负面行为（won't stand for such rudeness），是「容忍、容许」；② stand + out 是不及物用法，意为「突出、显眼」，后面常接 from / among；③ stand + up + for + 人，多一个 up，意为「维护、支持（他人）」，与 stand for 的「代表」完全不同；④ 主语是「物 + 地点」时是「坐落、矗立」。完形里这四个短语义各考各的，不能只背一个。

我给你拍照时站着别动。

批评开始的时候，她的同事中很少有人愿意站出来支持她。
state/steɪt/T1
考场判断法：① state 后面接 that 从句或 the reasons，是动词「陈述、说明」，主语常是专家、发言人；② 前面有 in 或 the state of，是名词「状况、状态」（in a poor state, the state of the environment）；③ 主语是国家名或美国州名，是「国家、州」；④ state-of-the-art 放在名词前作定语，意为「最先进的水准」。写作里表达「环境状况」写 the state of the environment，不要写 the environment condition。

国家；（美国的）州，一级行政区

member states 动词搭配
All member states agreed to the new rule.
所有成员国都同意这项新规定。
the United States 名词词块
He moved to the United States to study.
他搬到美国去读书。
state visit 动词搭配
The president made a state visit to France.
总统对法国进行了国事访问。
state-owned 动词搭配
The company is state-owned.
这家公司是国有的。

状况、状态；情势

in a poor state 介词短语
The old bridge is in a poor state and may have to be closed for repairs.
那座旧桥状况很差，可能必须关闭维修。
the state of the environment 名词词块
The old bridge is in a poor state and may have to be closed for repairs.
那座旧桥状况很差，可能必须关闭维修。
state of mind 动词+介词

那座旧桥状况很差，可能必须关闭维修。
in a state of shock 介词短语
The old bridge is in a poor state and may have to be closed for repairs.
那座旧桥状况很差，可能必须关闭维修。

陈述、说明；正式声明

state that 动词搭配
The scientist stated clearly that the data did not support the earlier conclusion.
这位科学家明确表示，数据并不支持先前的结论。
state the reasons 动词搭配
The scientist stated clearly that the data did not support the earlier conclusion.
这位科学家明确表示，数据并不支持先前的结论。
as stated earlier 动词搭配

这位科学家明确表示，数据并不支持先前的结论。
it is stated that 动词搭配
The scientist stated clearly that the data did not support the earlier conclusion.
这位科学家明确表示，数据并不支持先前的结论。

最先进的水准、前沿水平

state-of-the-art equipment 动词+介词
The laboratory uses state-of-the-art equipment that few other schools in the country can afford.
这间实验室使用的是最先进的设备，别的学校很难负担得起。
state-of-the-art design 动词+介词
The laboratory uses state-of-the-art equipment that few other schools in the country can afford.
这间实验室使用的是最先进的设备，别的学校很难负担得起。
a state-of-the-art lab 名词词块
The laboratory uses state-of-the-art equipment that few other schools in the country can afford.
这间实验室使用的是最先进的设备，别的学校很难负担得起。
subject/ˈsʌbdʒɪkt/T1
考场判断法：① be subject to + 名词（approval / change / stress）且没有过去分词，是形容词「易受…的、须经…的」；② be subjected to + 名词（abuse / pressure / treatment）用了 be + 过去分词，是被动「遭受」；③ the subject of / on the subject of 是「主题、话题」；④ 校园课程语境（my favourite subject）才是「科目」；⑤ 君主与人民的关系语境（the king and his subjects）是「臣民」。语法填空常考 subject to 与 subjected to 的分词区别。

主题、话题；讨论的对象

the subject of 名词词块
Rain was the subject of our conversation.
雨是我们谈话的话题。
on the subject of 介词短语
He said little on the subject of money.
关于钱这个话题他没说多少。
subject matter 动词搭配
The subject matter of the film is quite serious.
这部电影的主题相当严肃。
a sensitive subject 名词词块
Politics can be a sensitive subject at dinner.
政治在餐桌上可能是敏感话题。

swing/swɪŋ/T1
考场判断法：① 先看主语：the rope / the door / his arm → 「摆动」；the balance / public opinion / the result → 「转向、转变」；② 看到 swing at sb 或 swing a bat → 「挥击、猛打」；③ 看到 a swing in the balance / a swing in mood → 名词「大幅转变」；④ 注意词形 swing-swung-swung 是语法填空高频，名词复数 swings 是「秋千」，别当成动词第三人称。

摆动、摇动；来回摆动

swing back and forth 动词搭配
The garden gate swung back and forth in the wind.
花园大门在风中来回摆动。
swing in the wind 动词+介词
The lanterns swung in the wind all night.
灯笼在风里整夜摇晃。
swing open 动词搭配
The door swung open as we approached.
我们走近时门被吹开了。
set sth swinging 动词搭配
The child set the rope swinging.
孩子让绳子荡了起来。

转向、转变（形势与舆论）

swing against 动词+介词
Public opinion swung sharply against the plan as soon as the news broke.
消息一传出，公众舆论就迅速转向反对该计划。
swing to the other side 动词+介词
Public opinion swung sharply against the plan as soon as the news broke.
消息一传出，公众舆论就迅速转向反对该计划。
the balance swings 名词词块
Public opinion swung sharply against the plan as soon as the news broke.
消息一传出，公众舆论就迅速转向反对该计划。
swing wildly 动词搭配
Public opinion swung sharply against the plan as soon as the news broke.
消息一传出，公众舆论就迅速转向反对该计划。

大幅转变、转向（a swing in the balance）

a swing in the balance 名词词块
The election brought a clear swing in the balance towards the smaller parties.
这次选举使天平明显转向小党派。
a swing in mood 名词词块
The election brought a clear swing in the balance towards the smaller parties.
这次选举使天平明显转向小党派。
a swing in the polls 名词词块
The election brought a clear swing in the balance towards the smaller parties.
这次选举使天平明显转向小党派。

挥击、猛打

swing at sb 动词+介词
He swung at the ball twice but missed it both times in the first round.
他在第一轮中两次挥棒击球，两次都没有打中。
swing a bat 动词搭配
He swung at the ball twice but missed it both times in the first round.
他在第一轮中两次挥棒击球，两次都没有打中。

他在第一轮中两次挥棒击球，两次都没有打中。
swing back a punch 动词搭配
He swung at the ball twice but missed it both times in the first round.
他在第一轮中两次挥棒击球，两次都没有打中。

秋千（复数 swings）

on the swings 介词短语
The swings in the park were painted a bright yellow last spring.
公园里的秋千去年春天被漆成了明亮的黄色。
a set of swings 名词词块
The swings in the park were painted a bright yellow last spring.
公园里的秋千去年春天被漆成了明亮的黄色。
the playground swings 名词词块
The swings in the park were painted a bright yellow last spring.
公园里的秋千去年春天被漆成了明亮的黄色。
treat/triːt/T1
考场判断法：① treat 后面跟 to + 食物或地点（treat sb to dinner），是「请客、招待」，介词只能用 to，写成 treat sb for sth 直接错；② treat A as B，as 是关键信号，意为「把…视为」；③ treat 后接人 + 副词（treat sb well / badly / seriously），是「对待、看待」；④ treat 后接 for + 病名，或句中出现 doctor / hospital，是「治疗」。

对待、看待；处理

treat sb fairly 动词搭配
A good teacher treats every student fairly.
好老师公平对待每个学生。
treat sb badly 动词搭配
It is wrong to treat an animal badly.
虐待动物是错误的。
treat sth seriously 动词搭配
We should treat the warning seriously.
我们应认真对待这个警告。
treat the problem 动词搭配
How would you treat the problem of waste?
你会如何处理浪费问题？

治疗、医治；诊治

be treated for 系表搭配
She was treated for months before the doctors finally knew what was wrong.
她接受了数月的治疗，医生才最终弄清病因。
treat a disease 动词搭配

她接受了数月的治疗，医生才最终弄清病因。

She was treated for months before the doctors finally knew what was wrong.
她接受了数月的治疗，医生才最终弄清病因。

After the exam, my friends treated us all to a big dinner at the noodle shop.

把…视为、看作（treat as）

treat sth as 动词搭配
Many people still treat online learning as a second choice rather than a real alternative.
许多人仍把线上学习当作次选，而不是真正的替代方案。

value/ˈvæljuː/T1
考场判断法：① place / put value on sth，是「重视、看重」；② values 出现复数且前面有 moral / core / traditional，是「价值观」，不是「价格」；③ be of great value to sb，to 后面是人，是「对…很有价值、很有用」；④ value sth at + 金额，是「估价」；⑤ 谈价钱、性价比（good value for money）时才是名词「价值、价格」。应用文写「重视时间」用 place a high value on time。

价值、价钱；重要性

market value 动词搭配
The house has lost some of its market value.
这栋房的市场价值有所下降。

他明白努力工作的价值。
great value for money 动词+介词
This backpack is great value for money.
这个背包非常划算。
add value to 动词+介词
Good design adds value to a product.
好的设计为产品增加价值。

重视、看重；给予高度评价

place value on sth 动词+介词

懂得珍视自己的时间与精力，是一种好品质。

It is a good quality to place a high value on your own time and energy.

view/vju:/T1
考场判断法：① 一看到 view A as B 的框架，先判定它是动词「把…视为」，所有名词义全部出局；② 宾语是 of the 加地名或景物 → 「景色」；接 on / about → 「看法」；③ 出现 from a ... point of view → 「视角、着眼点」，不是「看法」；④ view 的被动式（has been viewed）多半是「观看、浏览」，学生按名词义读会整句译反。

依我看，这个计划需要更多时间。
a different view 名词词块
She has a different view on the issue.
她对这个问题有不同看法。
hold the view that 动词搭配
He holds the view that honesty matters most.
他认为诚实最重要。

anticipate/ænˈtɪsɪpeɪt/T2
考场判断法：① 看后面跟什么成分：跟 that 从句或名词（problems / changes / results）→ 「预料、预期」，是中性预测；跟动名词 doing（anticipate doing sth）→ 「期待、盼望」，带主观愿望色彩；② 结构上 anticipate 后面只能跟动名词，不能说 anticipate to do sth；③ 宾语是「需求、困难、变化」这类需要提前准备的东西（anticipate the needs / anticipate difficulties）→ 「预先想到」义；④ 看到句首 It is anticipated that… → 「据预计」，整块背，不要拆开逐字译。

category/ˈkætəgəri/T2
考场判断法：① 先看搭配：fall into the category of / be divided into categories → 「归入…类别」，这是高考数据、说明类文章最常见的说法；② 比较句里的 in a different category（altogether）不是「另一个类别」，而是「完全不是一回事」，后面常跟 altogether；③ 看有没有「高低档」的语境（the top category / the lowest category）→ 是「级别、档次」义，不是普通的「种类」；④ category 前面加 a 或 the，说明指的是一个具体的分类项；泛指「某一类事物」时说 a category of sth，搭配只能用 of。

a category of 名词词块
This is a special category of books for children.
这是一类特殊的儿童书籍。

The library groups books into four categories of size.
图书馆把书按大小分为四类。
one broad category 动词搭配
All these subjects fall into one broad category.
这些学科都归入一个大类。
classified into categories 动词+介词
The files were classified into categories.
文件被分门别类。

级别、档次（按高低排出的等次）

the top category 名词词块
The two designs are in completely different price categories, so they cannot be compared directly.
这两种设计完全处于不同的价格档次，因此无法直接比较。
the lowest category 名词词块

in a different category 介词短语

这两种设计完全处于不同的价格档次，因此无法直接比较。

与简答题相比，写小说完全是另一回事。

conflict/ˈkɒnflɪkt/T2
考场判断法：① 先看是名词还是动词：动词 conflict 后面几乎必跟 with（conflict with），意思是「与…抵触、不相容」；② 名词看宾语：a conflict between two sides / armed conflict → 冲突、战争；a conflict between two accounts / a conflict of evidence → 矛盾、分歧，译成「两说互相矛盾」而不是「战争」；③ a conflict of interest 必须整块背，译「利益冲突」，不能拆开逐字译；④ 阅读里主语常是 evidence / statements / versions，这时 conflict 一律是「矛盾」，不是「争执」。

冲突、争执（武装冲突、双方对立）

armed conflict 动词搭配
The armed conflict ended after two years.
武装冲突在两年后结束。
a conflict between 名词词块
There is a conflict between the two departments.
这两个部门之间存在冲突。

The two friends came into conflict over money.
这两个朋友因钱产生了冲突。
a local conflict 名词词块
The town was affected by a local conflict.
小镇受到一场局部冲突的影响。

矛盾、分歧（两种说法、证据之间）

a conflict between accounts 名词词块
There is a clear conflict between the two accounts of what happened that evening.
关于那天晚上发生了什么，两种说法之间存在明显矛盾。
a conflict of evidence 名词词块
There is a clear conflict between the two accounts of what happened that evening.
关于那天晚上发生了什么，两种说法之间存在明显矛盾。
an internal conflict 名词词块
There is a clear conflict between the two accounts of what happened that evening.
关于那天晚上发生了什么，两种说法之间存在明显矛盾。
conflicting versions 动词搭配
There is a clear conflict between the two accounts of what happened that evening.
关于那天晚上发生了什么，两种说法之间存在明显矛盾。

抵触、互不相容（conflict with）

conflict with 动词+介词
These two accounts of the same event conflict with each other in several important details.
关于同一事件的这两份说法在几个关键细节上互相抵触。
directly conflict 动词搭配
These two accounts of the same event conflict with each other in several important details.
关于同一事件的这两份说法在几个关键细节上互相抵触。

关于同一事件的这两份说法在几个关键细节上互相抵触。

These two accounts of the same event conflict with each other in several important details.
关于同一事件的这两份说法在几个关键细节上互相抵触。

（a conflict of interest）利益冲突

a conflict of interest 名词词块

有报道称她在授予合同一事上面临利益冲突后，这位官员辞去了职务。

convey/kənˈveɪ/T2
考场判断法：① 先看宾语是「信息、情感、意思」还是「货物、乘客、物资」：前者是「表达、传达」，后者是「运送、运输」；② 看到 convey a sense of / convey warmth / convey an impression → 「传达出、使人感到」，宾语是抽象的感觉或印象；③ 看到 convey sth to sb（convey my apologies to your family）→ 是「转达、捎带给某人」，介词 to 不能省，也不能换成 for；④ 注意 convey 不与 convince（说服）混：convince sb of sth / convince sb to do sth，意思完全不一样。

A smile can convey a meaning without words.
微笑无需言语就能传达意思。

音乐帮助人们传达无法表达的情感。
convey a message 动词搭配
The poster conveys a message of hope.
这张海报传达了希望的信息。
convey the impression 动词搭配
He conveyed the impression of being confident.
他给人留下自信的印象。

柔和的墙面颜色和温暖的光线向每位来访者传达出一种宁静感。

defeat/dɪˈfi:t/T2
考场判断法：① 先看是动词还是名词：动词 defeat 后面直接跟被击败的对象（they defeated their rivals）→ 「击败」；名词 a defeat / suffer a defeat → 「失败、败北」；② 宾语是 hope / plan / purpose / expectations → 「使落空、使破灭」，不是「击败」；③ 被动式 be defeated by 后面接对手，be defeated in 后面接比赛项目（They were defeated in the final），两个介词指向不同，别混；④ 注意 defeated 还能作形容词（a defeated team 被击败的队伍），语法填空考变形时不要写成 defeat。

击败、战胜（对手、队伍）

defeat sb 动词搭配
Our team defeated theirs in the final.
我们队在决赛中击败了他们队。
defeat the enemy 动词搭配
The soldiers defeated the enemy after a hard fight.
士兵们经过苦战击败了敌人。
defeat the champions 动词搭配
The newcomers defeated the champions in the first round.
新秀在第一轮击败了冠军。
defeat a team 动词搭配
They defeated a team much stronger than them.
他们击败了一支比他们强得多的队伍。

失败、败北（一场败仗）

a heavy defeat 名词词块
The defeat was painful, but the players still believed they had a chance next season.
这场失败令人痛苦，但队员们仍相信下个赛季还有机会。
suffer a defeat 动词搭配
The defeat was painful, but the players still believed they had a chance next season.
这场失败令人痛苦，但队员们仍相信下个赛季还有机会。
a crushing defeat 名词词块
The defeat was painful, but the players still believed they had a chance next season.
这场失败令人痛苦，但队员们仍相信下个赛季还有机会。
the defeat of the team 名词词块
The defeat was painful, but the players still believed they had a chance next season.
这场失败令人痛苦，但队员们仍相信下个赛季还有机会。

使（希望、计划）落空、挫败（努力）

defeat the purpose 动词搭配
A series of delays defeated all our plans for finishing the work before the holiday.
一连串延误让我们假期前完成工作的计划全部落空。
defeat one's hopes 动词搭配
A series of delays defeated all our plans for finishing the work before the holiday.
一连串延误让我们假期前完成工作的计划全部落空。
defeat their efforts 动词搭配
A series of delays defeated all our plans for finishing the work before the holiday.
一连串延误让我们假期前完成工作的计划全部落空。
defeat expectations 动词搭配
A series of delays defeated all our plans for finishing the work before the holiday.
一连串延误让我们假期前完成工作的计划全部落空。

（be defeated by）被…击败

be defeated by 系表搭配
The champion was defeated by a young player who had never won a professional match.
这位冠军被一名从未赢过职业比赛的年轻选手击败。
be narrowly defeated 系表搭配
The champion was defeated by a young player who had never won a professional match.
这位冠军被一名从未赢过职业比赛的年轻选手击败。

The champion was defeated by a young player who had never won a professional match.
这位冠军被一名从未赢过职业比赛的年轻选手击败。

The champion was defeated by a young player who had never won a professional match.
这位冠军被一名从未赢过职业比赛的年轻选手击败。
dominate/ˈdɒmɪneɪt/T2
考场判断法：① 先分词性：动词 dominate 后面必须带宾语（the market / the discussion），形容词放在名词前（a dominant position）；语法填空里看到 ___ position / ___ role 要填 dominant，不能填 dominate；② 宾语是「行业、市场、话题、局面」→ 「主导、支配」；宾语是「城堡、山脉、建筑物」且有从高处看的画面 → 「俯瞰、居高临下」；③ 宾语是心思、情绪（dominated by anxiety）→ 「支配、左右」；④ 写作里说「某公司主导市场」用 dominate the market，比 control the market 更贴高考语料。

主导、支配（占据主导地位、控制局面）

dominate the market 动词搭配
This brand dominates the market for phones.
这个品牌主导手机市场。
dominate the industry 动词搭配
A few companies dominate the industry.
少数几家公司主导这个行业。
dominate the discussion 动词搭配
He tended to dominate the discussion in class.
他常常主导课堂讨论。
dominate the landscape 动词搭配
Tall buildings dominate the city landscape.
高楼主导着城市景观。

占主导地位的、居首位的（多写作 dominant）

a dominant position 名词词块
Protecting the dominant species is essential if the whole ecosystem is to survive.
要保护整个生态系统得以存活，保护占主导地位的物种至关重要。
a dominant role 名词词块
Protecting the dominant species is essential if the whole ecosystem is to survive.

the dominant force 名词词块

play a dominant part 动词搭配
Protecting the dominant species is essential if the whole ecosystem is to survive.

这座古老石塔俯瞰整个山谷，几英里外都能看见。

a castle dominating the town 名词词块

这座古老石塔俯瞰整个山谷，几英里外都能看见。

支配、左右（心思、情绪）

dominated by fear 动词+介词

最终考试前漫长的一夜，焦虑一直左右着他的思绪。
dominate his thoughts 动词搭配

elegant/ˈelɪgənt/T2
考场判断法：① 先看被修饰的是什么：修饰人、举止、动作（an elegant woman / an elegant manner）→ 「优雅得体的」；修饰方案、思路、证明（an elegant solution / an elegant argument）→ 「简洁巧妙的」，高考里 elegant solution 就是「巧妙的办法」，不能只译成「漂亮的」；② 修饰服饰、家具、建筑（elegant furniture / an elegant design）→ 「雅致考究的」；③ 修饰文章、措辞（elegant writing / elegant wording）→ 「文笔优美、凝练的」；④ elegant 只能修饰「美而巧」的事物，用来形容笨拙、粗糙的方案在英语里不成立。

emerge/iˈmɜ:dʒ/T2
考场判断法：① 先看后面有没有 from：emerge from + 出处（the sea / the darkness / the ruins）→ 「从…中显现」；emerge from + 困境（emerge from a crisis / a difficult period）→ 「摆脱、走出来」；② 主语是抽象事物（a pattern / a trend / evidence / the truth emerges）→ 「浮现、显露出来」，不是「从暗处走出」；③ 看到 emerge as + 身份（emerge as the winner / emerge as a leader）→ 「崭露头角、最终成为」，as 后面一定接身份或角色；④ emerge 是不及物动词，中间不能夹宾语，不能写 emerge sth。

出现、显露（人或物现出来）

emerge from the darkness 动词+介词
A figure emerged from the darkness.
一个身影从黑暗中出现。
emerge from the sea 动词+介词
The island emerged from the sea long ago.
这座岛很久以前从海中浮现。
emerge from the ruins 动词+介词
A small flower emerged from the ruins.
一朵小花从废墟中冒出来。
emerge into view 动词+介词
The ship slowly emerged into view.
轮船慢慢出现在视野中。

浮现、显露出来（真相、迹象、趋势）

a pattern emerges 名词词块
As more data came in, a clear pattern emerged that surprised the whole research team.
随着更多数据到手，一个清晰的模式浮现出来，令整个研究团队都很惊讶。
the truth emerges 名词词块
As more data came in, a clear pattern emerged that surprised the whole research team.

evidence emerged 动词搭配

随着更多数据到手，一个清晰的模式浮现出来，令整个研究团队都很惊讶。
a trend emerges 名词词块
As more data came in, a clear pattern emerged that surprised the whole research team.
随着更多数据到手，一个清晰的模式浮现出来，令整个研究团队都很惊讶。

崭露头角、最终胜出（emerge as）

emerge as a leader 动词搭配

（emerge from）从…中显现；摆脱…出来

emerge from a crisis 动词+介词
The city slowly emerged from a long period of decline after the new policy took effect.
新政策生效后，这座城市慢慢从长期的萧条中走了出来。
emerge from the darkness 动词+介词
A figure emerged from the darkness.
一个身影从黑暗中出现。

exploit/ɪkˈsplɔɪt/T2
考场判断法：① 宾语是 energy / oil / coal / natural resources，是「开发、开采」；② 宾语是 advantages / potential / every opportunity，是「充分利用」；③ 主语是人或公司、宾语是 workers / cheap labour，且全句在批评，是贬义「剥削」，这是 E8 情感色彩考点；④ 宾语是具体的壮举（a remarkable exploit），是名词「功绩」。读到 exploit 不要一律想成贬义「剥削」。

充分发挥（优势、资源）；用足

exploit advantages 动词搭配
A good player knows how to exploit his advantages.
优秀的选手懂得如何发挥自己的优势。
exploit potential 动词搭配
The course helps students exploit their full potential.
这门课帮助学生充分发挥潜力。
fully exploit 动词搭配
We should fully exploit the resources we have.
我们应充分利用现有资源。
exploit every opportunity 动词搭配
She exploits every opportunity to practise English.
她抓住每一个机会练习英语。

开采、勘探（矿藏、能源）

exploit natural resources 动词搭配
The country is trying to exploit its solar energy without damaging the land.
这个国家正试图在不破坏土地的前提下开发其太阳能资源。
exploit solar energy 动词搭配

功绩、壮举；了不起的成就

a remarkable exploit 名词词块

facilitate/fəˈsɪlɪteɪt/T2
考场判断法：① 看宾语：抽象进程（communication / cooperation / integration / growth）→ 「促进、推动」；具体的事（access / the flow of traffic / the exchange of information）→ 「使…更方便、更顺畅」；人（facilitate sb's work）→ 「让某人做起来更容易」；② 结构上后面只能跟动名词：facilitate communication 是对的，facilitate sb to communicate 是错的，要表达「帮某人做」就写 facilitate sb's doing；③ 看到 facilitate 后面接名词而不是 doing，先问自己「这件事是不是变得更顺手了」；④ 写作里说「促进交流」用 facilitate communication，比 promote communication 更贴语境。

促进、推动（沟通、协作的展开）

facilitate communication 动词搭配
Translation apps facilitate communication between tourists.
翻译应用便利了游客之间的交流。
facilitate cooperation 动词搭配
The project facilitated cooperation between the two schools.
这个项目促进了两校之间的合作。
facilitate the process 动词搭配
The new system facilitates the process of applying.
新系统简化了申请流程。
facilitate the transition 动词搭配
Good teaching facilitates the transition to new topics.
好的教学有助于过渡到新主题。

使…更方便、更顺手（把事做起来更容易）

facilitate access 动词搭配
The new design facilitates the flow of traffic through the narrow market street.
新设计使车流在这条狭窄的市街上更顺畅。
facilitate the flow of 动词+介词
The new design facilitates the flow of traffic through the narrow market street.
新设计使车流在这条狭窄的市街上更顺畅。
facilitate the exchange of 动词+介词
The new design facilitates the flow of traffic through the narrow market street.
新设计使车流在这条狭窄的市街上更顺畅。
greatly facilitates 动词搭配
The new design facilitates the flow of traffic through the narrow market street.
新设计使车流在这条狭窄的市街上更顺畅。

帮（某人）做得更容易（facilitate sb's work）

facilitate sb's work 动词搭配
Digital notes greatly facilitate the work of teachers who prepare lessons for large classes.
数字化笔记极大地便利了为大批量班级备课的教师的工作。
facilitate communication with patients 动词+介词
Digital notes greatly facilitate the work of teachers who prepare lessons for large classes.

facilitate learning 动词搭配
Digital notes greatly facilitate the work of teachers who prepare lessons for large classes.
数字化笔记极大地便利了为大批量班级备课的教师的工作。
facilitate research 动词搭配
Digital notes greatly facilitate the work of teachers who prepare lessons for large classes.
数字化笔记极大地便利了为大批量班级备课的教师的工作。

（facilitate doing）后面只能接动名词

facilitate doing sth 动词搭配
The course is designed to facilitate the writing of clear academic reports.
这门课程旨在让学术报告写得更加清晰明了。
facilitate sb's doing sth 动词搭配
The course is designed to facilitate the writing of clear academic reports.
这门课程旨在让学术报告写得更加清晰明了。
to facilitate the exchange 介词短语
The course is designed to facilitate the writing of clear academic reports.
这门课程旨在让学术报告写得更加清晰明了。
facilitate communication 动词搭配
Translation apps facilitate communication between tourists.
翻译应用便利了游客之间的交流。
reform/rɪˈfɔ:m/T2
考场判断法：① 先看它是动词还是名词：动词后面直接跟宾语（reform the system / reform education）→ 「改革、变革」；名词前面有 the / a / major（a major reform / the reform）→ 「改革（措施）」；② 看宾语是什么：制度、学校、体制、工资 → 「改革、变革」；自己的坏习惯、错误做法、处世方式 → 「改正、矫正」，这一处最容易被误判；③ 看到 Reform and Opening-up 要整块背，译「改革开放」；④ 写作里说「改革某项制度」用 reform the system，比 change the system 更贴高考语料。

改革、变革（制度、教育、系统）

reform the system 动词搭配
The government promised to reform the system.
政府承诺改革这一制度。
reform education 动词搭配
The country decided to reform education in 2010.
这个国家在2010年决定改革教育。
reform the tax system 动词搭配
The government plans to reform the tax system.
政府计划改革税收制度。
reform and modernize 动词搭配
The factory had to reform and modernize its equipment.
工厂不得不改革并更新设备。

改革（名词用法；一项改革措施）

a major reform 名词词块
A major reform changed the way exams are marked.
一项重大改革改变了考试评分方式。
the reform of 名词词块
The new plan is a serious reform that will change the way the service is delivered.
新方案是一项重大改革，将会改变服务的提供方式。
carry out reform 动词搭配
The new plan is a serious reform that will change the way the service is delivered.
新方案是一项重大改革，将会改变服务的提供方式。
economic reform 动词搭配
The new plan is a serious reform that will change the way the service is delivered.
新方案是一项重大改革，将会改变服务的提供方式。

改正、纠正（坏习惯、错误做法）

reform oneself 动词搭配
The young man tried hard to reform his ways before the court agreed to let him go.
在法庭同意放他走之前，这年轻人努力改过自新。
reform one's ways 动词搭配
The young man tried hard to reform his ways before the court agreed to let him go.
在法庭同意放他走之前，这年轻人努力改过自新。
reform bad habits 动词搭配
The young man tried hard to reform his ways before the court agreed to let him go.
在法庭同意放他走之前，这年轻人努力改过自新。
reform the bad habits 动词搭配
The young man tried hard to reform his ways before the court agreed to let him go.
在法庭同意放他走之前，这年轻人努力改过自新。

（reform and opening-up）改革开放

reform and opening-up 动词搭配
Reform and opening-up has brought new choices to people in the small coastal towns as well.
改革开放也给沿海小城镇的人们带来了新的选择。
since reform and opening-up 动词搭配
Reform and opening-up has brought new choices to people in the small coastal towns as well.
改革开放也给沿海小城镇的人们带来了新的选择。
China's reform and opening-up 动词搭配
Reform and opening-up has brought new choices to people in the small coastal towns as well.
改革开放也给沿海小城镇的人们带来了新的选择。
the reform and opening-up process 名词词块
Reform and opening-up has brought new choices to people in the small coastal towns as well.
改革开放也给沿海小城镇的人们带来了新的选择。
spectacular/spekˈtækjələ(r)/T2
考场判断法：① 先看它修饰什么：修饰 scenery / view / sight / scene / fireworks → 「壮观、宏伟的」；修饰 success / performance / result / achievement → 「惊人的、极为出色的」，这时不能译成「壮观的」；② 看到 the spectacle / a spectacular 作名词 → 「壮观场面、盛况」，spectacle 本身也常作名词「壮观景象、奇观」；③ 固定词块 a spectacular success 记成「巨大的成功」，不要拆开；④ 它本身不含「成功」的意思，翻译时要靠宾语决定，别一律译「壮观的」。

壮观、宏伟的（景象、场面、风景）

a spectacular view 名词词块
We enjoyed a spectacular view from the mountain top.
我们在山顶欣赏到壮观的景色。
spectacular scenery 动词搭配
The region is famous for its spectacular scenery.
这个地区以壮丽风景闻名。
a spectacular waterfall 名词词块
The spectacular waterfall attracts many visitors.
这道壮观的瀑布吸引许多游客。
spectacular displays 动词搭配
The festival included spectacular displays of light.
节日包括壮观的光影表演。

惊人的、极为出色的（成功、演出）

a spectacular success 名词词块
The young team put on a spectacular performance that no one in the crowd would forget.
年轻队献上了一场令人难忘的精彩表演，观众中没有一个人会忘记。
spectacular results 动词搭配
The young team put on a spectacular performance that no one in the crowd would forget.
年轻队献上了一场令人难忘的精彩表演，观众中没有一个人会忘记。
a spectacular performance 名词词块
The young team put on a spectacular performance that no one in the crowd would forget.
年轻队献上了一场令人难忘的精彩表演，观众中没有一个人会忘记。
spectacular achievement 动词搭配
The young team put on a spectacular performance that no one in the crowd would forget.
年轻队献上了一场令人难忘的精彩表演，观众中没有一个人会忘记。

壮观场面、盛况（a spectacular）

a spectacle 名词词块
The opening ceremony of the games was a spectacle that a large number of people watched on screen.
运动会的开幕式是一场盛况，众多观众通过屏幕观看了它。
the spectacle 名词词块
The opening ceremony of the games was a spectacle that a large number of people watched on screen.
运动会的开幕式是一场盛况，众多观众通过屏幕观看了它。
watch the spectacle 动词搭配
The opening ceremony of the games was a spectacle that a large number of people watched on screen.
运动会的开幕式是一场盛况，众多观众通过屏幕观看了它。
a nightly spectacle 名词词块
The opening ceremony of the games was a spectacle that a large number of people watched on screen.
运动会的开幕式是一场盛况，众多观众通过屏幕观看了它。

（a spectacular success）巨大的成功、了不起的成绩

a spectacular success 名词词块
The young team put on a spectacular performance that no one in the crowd would forget.
年轻队献上了一场令人难忘的精彩表演，观众中没有一个人会忘记。
prove a spectacular success 动词搭配
The school's reading programme has proved a spectacular success, and more students now read outside class.
学校的阅读计划被证明取得了巨大成功，现在有更多学生在课外阅读。
an outright spectacular success 名词词块
The school's reading programme has proved a spectacular success, and more students now read outside class.
学校的阅读计划被证明取得了巨大成功，现在有更多学生在课外阅读。
a spectacular commercial success 名词词块
The school's reading programme has proved a spectacular success, and more students now read outside class.
学校的阅读计划被证明取得了巨大成功，现在有更多学生在课外阅读。
abandon/əˈbændən/T2
考场判断法：① 先圈宾语：plan / attempt / idea / hope → 「放弃」；children / family / hometown → 「抛弃、离弃」；a broken car / luggage → 「弃下、撇下」；② 看到 abandon oneself to + 情绪（grief / despair / anger）→ 「沉溺于」，是一整块固定搭配，后面必须接名词或动名词；③ 看到 abandoned 形容词（an abandoned car / an abandoned child）时，意思仍是「被遗弃的」，可以用它反过来判断动词是「弃下」还是「抛弃」；④ 语法填空常考变形：abandon 的过去式与过去分词都是 abandoned，拼写中只有一个 n，不要写成 abandonned。

放弃（计划、尝试、想法）

abandon a plan 动词搭配
They had to abandon the plan because of the rain.
因为下雨他们不得不放弃计划。
abandon an attempt 动词搭配
He abandoned the attempt after three failures.
三次失败后他放弃了尝试。
abandon the idea 动词搭配
She abandoned the idea of moving to another city.
她打消了搬到另一个城市的念头。
abandon hope 动词搭配
Even in difficulty, we should never abandon hope.
即使在困境中，我们也不应放弃希望。

抛弃、离弃（人、故土、伙伴）

abandon one's family 动词搭配
The charity helps children who have been abandoned by parents unable to support them.
这家慈善机构帮助那些被无力抚养的父母遗弃的孩子。
abandon his hometown 动词搭配
The charity helps children who have been abandoned by parents unable to support them.
这家慈善机构帮助那些被无力抚养的父母遗弃的孩子。
abandon a baby 动词搭配
The charity helps children who have been abandoned by parents unable to support them.
这家慈善机构帮助那些被无力抚养的父母遗弃的孩子。
feel abandoned 动词搭配
The charity helps children who have been abandoned by parents unable to support them.
这家慈善机构帮助那些被无力抚养的父母遗弃的孩子。

弃下、撇下（抛锚的车、行李）

abandon the car 动词搭配
When the car broke down on the empty road, the driver had to abandon it and walk to the nearest village.
车在空荡的路上抛锚后，驾驶员只能弃车步行到最近的村子。
abandon the luggage 动词搭配
When the car broke down on the empty road, the driver had to abandon it and walk to the nearest village.
车在空荡的路上抛锚后，驾驶员只能弃车步行到最近的村子。
abandon a vehicle 动词搭配
When the car broke down on the empty road, the driver had to abandon it and walk to the nearest village.
车在空荡的路上抛锚后，驾驶员只能弃车步行到最近的村子。
abandon ship 动词搭配
When the car broke down on the empty road, the driver had to abandon it and walk to the nearest village.
车在空荡的路上抛锚后，驾驶员只能弃车步行到最近的村子。

（abandon oneself to）放纵自己沉溺于（某种情绪）

abandon oneself to grief 动词+介词
In her grief she abandoned herself to sorrow and refused to speak to anyone for weeks.
悲痛之中，她放纵自己沉溺于哀伤，几周不肯跟任何人说话。
abandon oneself to despair 动词+介词
In her grief she abandoned herself to sorrow and refused to speak to anyone for weeks.
悲痛之中，她放纵自己沉溺于哀伤，几周不肯跟任何人说话。
abandon oneself to pleasure 动词+介词
In her grief she abandoned herself to sorrow and refused to speak to anyone for weeks.
悲痛之中，她放纵自己沉溺于哀伤，几周不肯跟任何人说话。
abandon oneself to anger 动词+介词
In her grief she abandoned herself to sorrow and refused to speak to anyone for weeks.
悲痛之中，她放纵自己沉溺于哀伤，几周不肯跟任何人说话。

经济与科研类文章高频
