accuse/əˈkju:z/T2
考场判断法：① 先分宾语：后面接「人」并且用 of → 「指控、控告」；后面接「制度、做法、作风」这类东西（accuse the school system of failing）→ 「指责、批评」，前者是法庭语境，后者是舆论语境；② 介词只能用 of：accuse sb of theft / of cheating 是对的，accuse sb for theft 是错的；跟 for 搭配的是 blame sb for sth；③ be accused of 后面必须用动名词（being accused of cheating），不能用动词原形；④ 写作里 accuse 要带具体内容（accuse sb of breaking the rules），光写 accuse sb 会被判为搭配不完整。

指控、控告（某人做了某事）

accuse sb of theft 动词+介词
He was accused of theft by his neighbour.
他被邻居指控盗窃。
accuse sb of cheating 动词+介词
The teacher accused him of cheating in the exam.
老师指控他考试作弊。
accuse sb of doing sth 动词+介词
They accused him of breaking the window.
他们指控他打破了窗户。
accuse sb of lying 动词+介词
Do not accuse me of lying without proof.
没有证据不要指控我撒谎。

指责、抨击（某种做法、作风）

accuse the system of failing 动词+介词
Parents often accuse the school system of failing to give their children enough attention.
家长常指责学校制度没能给孩子足够的关注。
accuse a practice of being unfair 动词+介词
Parents often accuse the school system of failing to give their children enough attention.
家长常指责学校制度没能给孩子足够的关注。
accuse sb of ignoring 动词+介词
Parents often accuse the school system of failing to give their children enough attention.
家长常指责学校制度没能给孩子足够的关注。
accuse the company of cheating 动词+介词
Parents often accuse the school system of failing to give their children enough attention.
家长常指责学校制度没能给孩子足够的关注。

（be accused of）被告发、被指责于

be accused of cheating 系表搭配
Three men were accused of breaking into the laboratory, but the evidence against them was weak.
三人被指控闯入实验室，但针对他们的证据并不充分。
be accused of theft 系表搭配
Three men were accused of breaking into the laboratory, but the evidence against them was weak.
三人被指控闯入实验室，但针对他们的证据并不充分。
be accused of murder 系表搭配
Three men were accused of breaking into the laboratory, but the evidence against them was weak.
三人被指控闯入实验室，但针对他们的证据并不充分。
be accused of lying 系表搭配
Three men were accused of breaking into the laboratory, but the evidence against them was weak.
三人被指控闯入实验室，但针对他们的证据并不充分。

搭配在议论文出现

使一致、对齐；结盟

allocate/ˈæləˌkeɪt/T1
搭配在经济类文章高频

分配资源、时间

apart/əˈpɑ:t/T2
考场判断法：① apart 是副词，不单独作形容词用，看到 people apart / the two towns apart 一律按「彼此隔开」理解；② 判断它属于哪个词块：fall apart / come apart → 散架、裂开；tear apart → 撕碎、拆散；set apart → 使突出、使与众不同；apart from → 除…之外；③ apart from 有两种读法：后面接「除了它以外一切都还好」的一整件事 → 「除…之外」，后面接地点或距离 → 「与…相隔」；④ far apart、miles apart 中间必须有形容词或数词，不能只说 they apart。

分开地、彼此隔开（相隔一段距离）

far apart 动词搭配
The two villages are far apart in the mountains.
这两个村子在山里相距很远。
miles apart 动词搭配
The two schools are miles apart.
这两所学校相隔数英里。
well apart 动词搭配
Keep the two desks well apart in the room.
把这两张桌子在房间里分开放。
apart from each other 动词+介词
The twins are hard to tell apart from each other.
这对双胞胎很难区分。

散架、裂成碎块（结构坏了）

fall apart 动词搭配
The old wooden chair fell apart the moment he sat down on it.
他刚一坐下，那把旧木椅就散架了。
come apart 动词搭配
The old wooden chair fell apart the moment he sat down on it.
他刚一坐下，那把旧木椅就散架了。
fall to pieces 动词+介词
The old wooden chair fell apart the moment he sat down on it.
他刚一坐下，那把旧木椅就散架了。
the roof came apart 名词词块
The old wooden chair fell apart the moment he sat down on it.
他刚一坐下，那把旧木椅就散架了。

（set sb/sth apart）使突出、与众不同

set sb apart from 动词+介词
What set her apart from the other runners was her steady pace in the last kilometre.
让她与其他跑者拉开差距的是最后一公里稳健的配速。
set apart from 动词+介词
What set her apart from the other runners was her steady pace in the last kilometre.
让她与其他跑者拉开差距的是最后一公里稳健的配速。

（apart from）除…之外；与…相隔

apart from that 动词+介词
Apart from a few minor errors, her answer was clear and complete enough to earn full marks.
除了几处小错误，她的答案清楚又完整，足以拿满分。
apart from these 动词+介词
Apart from a few minor errors, her answer was clear and complete enough to earn full marks.
除了几处小错误，她的答案清楚又完整，足以拿满分。
quite apart from 动词+介词
Apart from a few minor errors, her answer was clear and complete enough to earn full marks.
除了几处小错误，她的答案清楚又完整，足以拿满分。
apart from each other 动词+介词
The twins are hard to tell apart from each other.
这对双胞胎很难区分。

（tear sth apart）撕碎、拆散

tear sth apart 动词搭配
The old letter had been torn apart by careless hands long before anyone found it.
在有人发现它之前，这封旧信早已被粗心的人撕成了碎片。

assemble/əˈsembl/T2
考场判断法：① 看宾语：parts / frame / machine / model → 「组装、装配」；a team / a committee / a working group → 「组建、召集」；evidence / data / information → 「收集、汇总」；② 主语是一群人、句子讲「大家聚到某个地方」（The students assembled in the hall）→ 才是「集合、聚集」；讲「把人力组织起来」时是「组建」，不是「集合」；③ 讲实物拼装的句子多用被动或完成时（has been fully assembled / is assembled by hand）；④ assemble 比 gather 更强调「拼成一个整体」，写作里组建团队用 assemble a team。

学生们在大厅集合开会。

队员们在比赛开始前集合。
the crowd assembled 名词词块

人群聚集起来观看游行。

assemble a team 动词搭配

来自不同大学的研究者在得出结论前汇总了数百项已发表研究中的证据。

老师用多种方式评估表现。
assign/əˈsaɪn/T2
考场判断法：① 先看宾语是「物」还是「人」：物（tasks / rooms / time / money）→ 「分配」；人（a teacher / him / two players）→ 「指派、委派」；② 宾语是「作业、任务、阅读材料」→ 「布置」；③ 宾语是 cause / blame / responsibility → 「把…归因于、把责任归于」，这是学术评论里的高频用法；④ 固定语序：assign sth to sb（把事派给人）与 assign sb to sth（把人派到事上）方向相反，介词决定谁给谁，不能互换。

分配、分派（任务、房间、经费）

assign a task 动词搭配
The teacher assigned a task to each group.
老师给每个小组分配了任务。
assign a room 动词搭配
The hotel assigned us a room on the third floor.
酒店给我们安排了三楼的一个房间。
assign a budget 动词搭配
The school assigned a budget to each club.
学校给每个社团分配了预算。
assign roles 动词搭配
The director assigned roles to the actors.
导演给演员分配了角色。

指派、委派（某人担任某职、做某事）

assign sb to a task 动词+介词
The head coach assigned two experienced players to lead the younger team this season.
主教练指派了两名经验丰富的球员本赛季带领年轻队。
assign sb to lead 动词+介词
The head coach assigned two experienced players to lead the younger team this season.
主教练指派了两名经验丰富的球员本赛季带领年轻队。
assign a teacher to a class 动词+介词
The head coach assigned two experienced players to lead the younger team this season.
主教练指派了两名经验丰富的球员本赛季带领年轻队。
assign sb a duty 动词搭配
The head coach assigned two experienced players to lead the younger team this season.
主教练指派了两名经验丰富的球员本赛季带领年轻队。

布置（作业、任务、阅读材料）

assign homework 动词搭配
The teacher assigned a short writing task for the weekend and collected the results on Monday.
老师布置了一个周末的短写作任务，并在周一收齐了结果。
assign a writing task 动词搭配
The teacher assigned a short writing task for the weekend and collected the results on Monday.
老师布置了一个周末的短写作任务，并在周一收齐了结果。
assign work 动词搭配
The teacher assigned a short writing task for the weekend and collected the results on Monday.
老师布置了一个周末的短写作任务，并在周一收齐了结果。
assign reading 动词搭配
The teacher assigned a short writing task for the weekend and collected the results on Monday.
老师布置了一个周末的短写作任务，并在周一收齐了结果。

把…归因于、把责任归于

assign the blame 动词搭配
Few readers would assign the rise in online shopping to any single cause.
很少有读者会把网购的兴起归因于某一种单一原因。
assign a cause 动词搭配
Few readers would assign the rise in online shopping to any single cause.
很少有读者会把网购的兴起归因于某一种单一原因。
assign responsibility to sb 动词+介词
Few readers would assign the rise in online shopping to any single cause.
很少有读者会把网购的兴起归因于某一种单一原因。
assign the rise to 动词+介词
Few readers would assign the rise in online shopping to any single cause.
很少有读者会把网购的兴起归因于某一种单一原因。

attempt to do 动词+介词

attribute/əˈtrɪˌbjut/T1

bond/bɒnd/T2
考场判断法：① 句中提到 issue / sell / interest rate / government，且主语是机构，是金融义「债券」；② 主语是人、后面接 with sb，是「纽带、情感联系」；③ 动词 bond with sb / bond over sth，是「建立、增强（信任）关系」，主语常是共同经历或活动；④ 注意 bond 既是名词也是动词，语法填空常在名词与动词之间转换。

该市发行债券来支付新医院和学校体育馆的建造费用。
a ten-year bond 名词词块
The city issued bonds to pay for the new hospital and the school gym.
该市发行债券来支付新医院和学校体育馆的建造费用。

一起做项目让两个班的关系比任何讲座都紧密得多。

一起做项目让两个班的关系比任何讲座都紧密得多。

captain/ˈkæptɪn/T2
考场判断法：① 先找场景线索：跟着 team / side / players / squad → 运动队队长；跟着 ship / vessel / boat / crew → 船长；跟着 plane / flight / cockpit → 机长；② 看到 a fishing boat、a cargo ship 这类船只词，直接锁定船长义，不要一律读成「队长」；③ 看到 flight / cockpit / landing 语境就是机长；④ 句首大写的 Captain 作称呼时是对船长的尊称，不表示能力高低。

她被选为队长。

她当了三年队长。
a national captain 名词词块
He became a national captain at a young age.
他年纪轻轻就成了国家队长。

渔船船长见海浪比往常更高，决定掉头返航。

contest/ˈkɒntest/T2
考场判断法：① 先看它作名词还是动词——作名词且前面有 a singing / writing / beauty contest 这类修饰，就是「赛事、比赛活动」；② 动词后面接的是「说法、决定、结果、权利」这类抽象内容（contest a claim / contest the decision）→ 必是「质疑、提出异议」，不是「参加比赛」；③ 动词后面接的是「名次、奖项、职位、冠军」这类可以争取的名位（contest for the title / contest the championship）→ 才是「参加竞逐」；④ 出现 a matter of contest 这类固定说法时，译成「争议之事」，别译成「比赛」。

赛事、比赛活动

a writing contest 名词词块
She entered a writing contest at school.
她参加了学校的写作比赛。

criterion/kraɪˈtɪəriən/T2
考场判断法：① 看到 criterion 先数数：单数 a criterion for… 是「一项标准、判据」，复数必须写 criteria，绝不能写 criterions；② 句子在谈评分、评估、数据（the criteria for measuring performance），是「指标、评判尺度」，不是抽象的「原则」；③ 谈人生、道德、价值判断（a criterion for a good life），是抽象的标准、尺度。应用文写评选标准用 criteria for (doing) sth。

衡量优劣的依据；评判的尺度

a criterion for 名词词块
Cost is an important criterion for choosing a phone.
价格是选手机的一个重要标准。
the criterion of 名词词块
The criterion of success here is clear.
这里成功的标准很明确。
meet the criteria 动词搭配
Only two applicants met the criteria.
只有两名申请者符合标准。
apply a criterion 动词搭配
We applied the same criterion to every case.
我们对每个案例都采用同一标准。

指标、判据；可衡量的尺度

criteria for measuring 动词+介词
The criteria for measuring progress must be set before the project begins, not afterwards.
衡量进展的指标必须在项目开始之前确定，而不是事后。
selection criteria 动词搭配
The criteria for measuring progress must be set before the project begins, not afterwards.
衡量进展的指标必须在项目开始之前确定，而不是事后。
performance criteria 动词搭配
The criteria for measuring progress must be set before the project begins, not afterwards.
衡量进展的指标必须在项目开始之前确定，而不是事后。
evaluation criteria 动词搭配
The criteria for measuring progress must be set before the project begins, not afterwards.
衡量进展的指标必须在项目开始之前确定，而不是事后。

多项指标汇总清单（复数 criteria）

criteria were set 动词搭配

多项标准事先公布，这样日后谁也无法再为此争论。
the criteria listed 名词词块
Several criteria were published in advance so that no one could argue about them later.
多项标准事先公布，这样日后谁也无法再为此争论。
on these criteria 介词短语
Several criteria were published in advance so that no one could argue about them later.
多项标准事先公布，这样日后谁也无法再为此争论。
meet all the criteria 动词搭配

多项标准事先公布，这样日后谁也无法再为此争论。

division/dɪˈvɪʒn/T2
考场判断法：① 先看介词组合：between / within 后面是人或群体，是「分歧、不和」；② 前面有 business / sales / marketing / finance 这类行业名，是「部门、科室」；③ 后面接 labour（the division of labour），是「分工」；④ 数学语境（long division, division sign），是「除法」；⑤ 其余「把…分成」的语境才是「分开、划分」。别把 division 一律读成「分开」，高考里读成「分歧、部门」更常见。

把整体拆成若干部分；由此形成的结果

the division of 名词词块
The division of labour makes work faster.
分工使工作更快。
a clear division 名词词块
There is a clear division between the two tasks.
这两项任务之间有明确分工。
line of division 动词+介词

这条河形成了天然的分界线。
division into 动词+介词
The class was divided into four groups.
全班被分成四组。

双方之间深深的分歧使谈判几乎无法继续。

Deep divisions between the two sides made the negotiation almost impossible to continue.
双方之间深深的分歧使谈判几乎无法继续。

双方之间深深的分歧使谈判几乎无法继续。
division within the team 动词搭配

双方之间深深的分歧使谈判几乎无法继续。

Pupils usually learn long division only after they have mastered basic multiplication.
学生通常在掌握基本乘法之后才学长除法。

分工；劳动的分工（labour）

the division of labour 名词词块
Specialization grows because the division of labour lets each worker master a smaller set of skills.
分工让每个工人只需掌握更少的技能，专业化因此不断加深。

exchange/ɪksˈtʃeɪndʒ/T2
考场判断法：① 先看宾语：实物（gifts / presents / phone numbers / seats）→ 「互换」；言语、目光（words / glances / greetings）→ 「交谈、互致」；② 名词 exchange 前面有 a student / cultural / international → 「交流、往来（项目、人）」；③ 看到 rate / currency / foreign → 「汇率、外币兑换」，这一块必须整块记；④ in exchange for（以…换取）后面接被换出去的东西，in return（作为回报）后面接得到的东西，两个介词短语的位置不能互换。

互换、交换（礼物、号码、意见）

exchange gifts 动词搭配
Family members exchange gifts at Christmas.
家人在圣诞节互赠礼物。
exchange phone numbers 动词搭配
They exchanged phone numbers after the meeting.
会后他们交换了电话号码。
exchange information 动词搭配
The two teams exchanged information before the match.
两队赛前交换了信息。
exchange places 动词搭配
The two boys exchanged places in the line.
两个男孩在队伍里换了位置。

交流、往来（人、项目、看法）

a student exchange 名词词块

她通过交换生项目在国外待了一年，这改变了她看待学习的方式。

She spent a year abroad on a student exchange that changed the way she looked at learning.
她通过交换生项目在国外待了一年，这改变了她看待学习的方式。
exchange students 动词搭配
She spent a year abroad on a student exchange that changed the way she looked at learning.
她通过交换生项目在国外待了一年，这改变了她看待学习的方式。
in exchange for 介词短语
She spent a year abroad on a student exchange that changed the way she looked at learning.
她通过交换生项目在国外待了一年，这改变了她看待学习的方式。

汇率变动太快，许多旅行者只在回国当天才去兑换货币。

indicate/ˈɪndɪkeɪt/T1
考场判断法：① 主语是研究、报告、数据、迹象（studies, results, data, signs）时，indicate that 加从句＝「表明」，后面通常跟结论；② 主语是指路牌、箭头、老师的手势 → 「指示、指出方向」；③ 主语是颜色、符号、手势这类象征物 → 「象征、意味着」；④ 语法填空的固定考法是 indicate that 加从句，别漏掉 that。

表明、显示（研究或迹象指向某个结论）

indicate that 动词搭配
The survey indicates that most students sleep too little.
调查表明大多数学生睡眠不足。
the results indicate 名词词块
The results indicate a clear improvement.
结果表明有明显进步。
indicate a trend 动词搭配
The figures indicate a trend towards online shopping.
这些数字显示出网购的趋势。
the figures indicate 名词词块
The figures indicate that sales have slowed.
数字显示销售放缓了。

indicate the way 动词搭配

拐角处的一块木牌指示着通往古寺的小路。
indicate the direction 动词搭配
A wooden sign at the corner indicated the path leading to the old temple.
拐角处的一块木牌指示着通往古寺的小路。
indicate a turn 动词搭配

拐角处的一块木牌指示着通往古寺的小路。
indicate clearly 动词搭配
A wooden sign at the corner indicated the path leading to the old temple.
拐角处的一块木牌指示着通往古寺的小路。

nation/ˈneɪʃn/T2
考场判断法：① 先看后置修饰：修饰国家、地理（an island nation / a wealthy nation）→ 「国家」；修饰人群、共同身份（a nation of readers / a proud nation）→ 「民族、群体」；② the nation 单独作主语时指「全国国民」，译成「全国人民」，不要译成「这个国家」；③ 看到 United Nations 必须整块记，译「联合国」，是大写专名，不是「联合的国家们」；④ country 侧重国土与政权，nation 侧重「有共同身份与文化的人群」，所以一个国家可以是一个不大的民族。

国家（政治意义上的独立国家）

a developed nation 名词词块
Japan is a highly developed nation.
日本是一个高度发达的国家。
an island nation 名词词块
New Zealand is an island nation.
新西兰是一个岛国。
a wealthy nation 名词词块
A wealthy nation should help poorer ones.
富裕国家应帮助较贫穷的国家。
the whole nation 名词词块
The whole nation celebrated the victory.
举国上下庆祝胜利。

民族（共享身份与文化的人群）

a nation of readers 名词词块
The country is home to a nation of skilled craftsmen whose craft has been handed down for generations.
这个国家生活着一群手艺精湛的工匠，他们的手艺代代相传。
a proud nation 名词词块
The country is home to a nation of skilled craftsmen whose craft has been handed down for generations.
这个国家生活着一群手艺精湛的工匠，他们的手艺代代相传。
the whole nation 名词词块
The whole nation celebrated the victory.
举国上下庆祝胜利。
one nation 动词搭配
The country is home to a nation of skilled craftsmen whose craft has been handed down for generations.
这个国家生活着一群手艺精湛的工匠，他们的手艺代代相传。

国民、民众（the nation 指全体国民）

the nation as a whole 名词词块
The government hopes that the whole nation will take part in the clean-up campaign.
政府希望全国人民都参加这次清洁行动。
the whole nation 名词词块
The whole nation celebrated the victory.
举国上下庆祝胜利。
the nation should 名词词块
The government hopes that the whole nation will take part in the clean-up campaign.
政府希望全国人民都参加这次清洁行动。
serve the nation 动词搭配
The government hopes that the whole nation will take part in the clean-up campaign.
政府希望全国人民都参加这次清洁行动。

（the United Nations）联合国

the United Nations 名词词块
The United Nations has called on governments to protect the rights of children in crisis areas.
联合国呼吁各国政府保护危机地区儿童的权利。
a United Nations report 名词词块
The United Nations has called on governments to protect the rights of children in crisis areas.
联合国呼吁各国政府保护危机地区儿童的权利。
United Nations officials 动词搭配
The United Nations has called on governments to protect the rights of children in crisis areas.
联合国呼吁各国政府保护危机地区儿童的权利。
under the United Nations 介词短语
The United Nations has called on governments to protect the rights of children in crisis areas.
联合国呼吁各国政府保护危机地区儿童的权利。

object/ˈɒbdʒɪkt/T1

observe/əbˈzɜ:v/T1
考场判断法：① 看宾语：the rules / safety rules / the treaty / a deadline → 「遵守、遵循」；a plant / a change / how things develop → 「观察、注意到」；② 出现节日名 + is observed on → 「庆祝、纪念」，这是传统节日话题的固定说法；③ 后面接 that 从句且主语是人时，observe that 是「指出、评论说」，不是「观察到」；④ observe 是及物动词，后接宾语或 that 从句，不能说 observe to do。

观察、注视；注意到

observe a change 动词搭配
Scientists observed a change in the bird’s behaviour.
科学家观察到这种鸟的行为变化。
observe that 动词搭配
He observed that the children were quietly reading.
他注意到孩子们在安静地阅读。
observe carefully 动词搭配
Observe carefully before you draw a conclusion.
下结论前要仔细观察。
observe the behaviour of 动词+介词
Scientists observe the behaviour of the birds.
科学家观察这些鸟的行为。

遵守、遵循（规则与协议）

observe the rules 动词搭配
Everyone must observe the rules during the exam.
考试期间每个人都必须遵守规则。
observe safety rules 动词搭配
All visitors must observe the safety rules posted at the entrance of the hall.
所有参观者都必须遵守大厅入口处张贴的安全规定。
observe a deadline 动词搭配
All visitors must observe the safety rules posted at the entrance of the hall.
所有参观者都必须遵守大厅入口处张贴的安全规定。
observe the treaty 动词搭配
All visitors must observe the safety rules posted at the entrance of the hall.
所有参观者都必须遵守大厅入口处张贴的安全规定。

庆祝、纪念（节日与周年）

be observed on 系表搭配
The festival is observed on the ninth day of the second lunar month.
这个节日在农历二月初九庆祝。
the holiday is observed 名词词块
The festival is observed on the ninth day of the second lunar month.
这个节日在农历二月初九庆祝。
observed with great ceremony 动词+介词
The festival is observed on the ninth day of the second lunar month.
这个节日在农历二月初九庆祝。

指出、评论说（that 从句）

observe that 动词搭配
He observed that the children were quietly reading.
他注意到孩子们在安静地阅读。
it was observed that 动词搭配
He observed that the problem was not simply a lack of money.
他指出，问题并不只是缺钱那么简单。
as one observer noted 动词搭配
He observed that the problem was not simply a lack of money.
他指出，问题并不只是缺钱那么简单。
occupy/ˈɒkjupaɪ/T1
考场判断法：① 宾语是 room / seat / space → 「占据」；宾语是 time / energy / attention → 「占用」；② 看到 be occupied with / be occupied in，记成「忙于」而不是「被占满」，这是完形填空最常考的熟词僻义；③ 宾语是城市、领土、国家 → 「占领」；④ occupied 作形容词时看后面跟 with 还是跟地点，才知道是「忙于」还是「被占用」。

占据（空间、位置、席位）

occupy a seat 动词搭配
Please occupy a seat before the show starts.
演出开始前请就座。
occupy space 动词搭配
The big sofa occupies too much space in the room.
这张大沙发占了房间太多空间。
occupy a place in 动词+介词
Music occupies an important place in her life.
音乐在她生活中占重要地位。
occupy the front row 动词搭配
The teachers occupied the front row.
老师们坐在前排。

占用（时间、精力、资源）

occupy most of the time 动词+介词
Preparing for the final exam occupied most of her free time this term.
准备期末考试占据了她这学期大部分空闲时间。
occupy my attention 动词搭配
Preparing for the final exam occupied most of her free time this term.
准备期末考试占据了她这学期大部分空闲时间。
occupy the resources 动词搭配
Preparing for the final exam occupied most of her free time this term.
准备期末考试占据了她这学期大部分空闲时间。
fully occupy 动词搭配
Preparing for the final exam occupied most of her free time this term.
准备期末考试占据了她这学期大部分空闲时间。

忙于、使…忙碌（无暇分身）

be occupied with sth 系表搭配
She was occupied with preparing a short speech for the school festival next week.
她正忙着准备下周校庆会上的简短演讲。
be occupied in doing 系表搭配
She was occupied with preparing a short speech for the school festival next week.
她正忙着准备下周校庆会上的简短演讲。
be fully occupied 系表搭配
She was occupied with preparing a short speech for the school festival next week.
她正忙着准备下周校庆会上的简短演讲。
keep sb occupied 动词搭配
She was occupied with preparing a short speech for the school festival next week.
她正忙着准备下周校庆会上的简短演讲。

占领（城市、领土）

occupy the town 动词搭配
The enemy occupied the coastal town for nearly a month before withdrawing.
敌军占领了这座海滨城镇近一个月后才撤退。
be occupied by troops 系表搭配
The enemy occupied the coastal town for nearly a month before withdrawing.
敌军占领了这座海滨城镇近一个月后才撤退。
occupy the country 动词搭配
The enemy occupied the coastal town for nearly a month before withdrawing.
敌军占领了这座海滨城镇近一个月后才撤退。
military occupation 动词搭配
The enemy occupied the coastal town for nearly a month before withdrawing.
敌军占领了这座海滨城镇近一个月后才撤退。

prove/pru:v/T1
考场判断法：① 先看宾语是不是「命题、说法、理论」——是则是「证明、证实」；② 宾语是人或品质（courage、ability、oneself）→ 「展现、显出」；③ 后面是 to be 加名词或形容词，或者 prove 加形容词（prove difficult / prove useful），结果是「结果是…、原来是…」，不必真有证据；④ 语法填空里 prove to be 是不加 to 不加 be 的高频结构，只写 prove 拿不到分。

证明、证实（用证据说明命题成立）

prove that 动词搭配
The evidence proved that he was innocent.
证据证明他是无辜的。
prove the theory 动词搭配
The experiment proved the theory.
这个实验证明了该理论。
prove a claim 动词搭配
The data proved the claim to be true.
数据证明这一说法属实。
prove to be true 动词+介词
His words proved to be true in the end.
他的话最终证明是真的。

展现、显出（某种品质或实力）

prove one's worth 动词搭配
The young athlete proved her courage by finishing the race despite the injury.
这位年轻运动员不顾伤病跑完全程，展现了勇气。
prove oneself 动词搭配
The young athlete proved her courage by finishing the race despite the injury.
这位年轻运动员不顾伤病跑完全程，展现了勇气。
prove his ability 动词搭配
The young athlete proved her courage by finishing the race despite the injury.
这位年轻运动员不顾伤病跑完全程，展现了勇气。
prove true 动词搭配
The young athlete proved her courage by finishing the race despite the injury.
这位年轻运动员不顾伤病跑完全程，展现了勇气。

结果证明是、原来是

prove to be 动词+介词
The task proved to be far harder than expected.
这项任务证明比预想难得多。
prove difficult 动词搭配
What looked like a serious problem proved to be only a temporary setback.
看似严重的问题结果证明只是一次暂时的挫折。
prove useful 动词搭配
The old map proved useful during the hike.
那张旧地图在徒步中证明很有用。
prove to be true 动词+介词
His words proved to be true in the end.
他的话最终证明是真的。

replace/rɪˈpleɪs/T1
考场判断法：① 先圈出「被换掉的」和「换进来的」两样东西；② 两样都是人且谈职位 → 「接替」；两样都是同类物品、一新一旧 → 「更换」；③ 看到 replace A with B 就固定读成「用 B 换掉 A」，而 replace A by B 是「用 A 换掉 B」，介词一写反整句意思就倒过来，这是 E4 级硬伤；④ 作文写「取代旧做法」优先用 replace the old system、replace A with B 这类高考现成搭配。

取代、顶替（原有的人或物被换掉）

replace the old system 动词搭配
The school replaced the old system last year.
学校去年更换了旧制度。
be replaced by 系表搭配
Handwritten notes have been replaced by typed ones.
手写笔记已被打字笔记取代。
completely replace 动词搭配
Digital photos have not completely replaced film.
数码照片并未完全取代胶片。

更换（把旧的换成新的同类物）

replace the filter 动词搭配
Remember to replace the filter every month.
记得每月更换滤芯。
replace a worn-out part 动词搭配
Our school replaces the lamps in the reading room every second year to save electricity.
学校每两年更换一次阅览室的灯，以节约用电。
replace the bulb 动词搭配
Our school replaces the lamps in the reading room every second year to save electricity.
学校每两年更换一次阅览室的灯，以节约用电。

接替（接任某人的职位或角色）

replace sb as manager 动词搭配
When the head coach stepped down, a young assistant replaced him at short notice.
主教练卸任后，一位年轻助理在短时间内接替了他。
replace him as coach 动词搭配
When the head coach stepped down, a young assistant replaced him at short notice.
主教练卸任后，一位年轻助理在短时间内接替了他。
be replaced by 系表搭配
Handwritten notes have been replaced by typed ones.
手写笔记已被打字笔记取代。

用…替换（甲换乙）

replace A with B 动词+介词
Replace the old batteries with new ones.
把旧电池换成新的。
replace the old with the new 动词+介词
The school replaced the old blackboards with smart screens that record every lesson.
学校用可记录每节课的智能屏幕替换了旧黑板。
replace the text with a photo 动词+介词
The school replaced the old blackboards with smart screens that record every lesson.
学校用可记录每节课的智能屏幕替换了旧黑板。

settle/ˈsetl/T1
考场判断法：① 宾语是 dispute / conflict / difference 这类「争端类」→ 「解决、和解」；② 宾语是 date / price / details 这类「事务类」→ 「敲定、确定」；③ 后面不接宾语或跟 in / into → 「定居、安顿」，settle down 这个固定块是「安顿下来、平静下来」，别按「解决」读。

suspect/səˈspekt/T1
考场判断法：① suspect 后面接 of + 人或 that 从句且涉及「罪行」→ 「怀疑有嫌疑」；② suspect that 后面说的是自己不确定的事 → 「猜想、料想」，语气是中性推测；③ 冠词 the + suspect 且指人 → 名词「嫌疑人」；④ 修饰 motive / excuse / package → 形容词「可疑的」。suspect 横跨 v / n / adj 三种词性，先定词性再定义项。

怀疑、觉得…有嫌疑

警方怀疑他偷了钱。

我怀疑他知道的不止他说的。

他被怀疑协助了小偷。
a suspected case 名词词块
Doctors reported a suspected case of the disease.
医生报告了一例疑似病例。

suspend/səˈspend/T2
考场判断法：① 宾语是计划、服务、业务、判决这类「正在进行的事」，是「暂停、中止、推迟」，这是高考最常考的义项；② 宾语是灯、画、布帘等具体物，且后面有 from（suspend a lamp from the ceiling），是「悬挂」；③ 主语是人且出现在机构场景（a player was suspended / an official was suspended），是「停职、除名」；④ 宾语是颗粒、尘埃（remain suspended in the air），是「悬浮」。写应用文表达「暂停服务」固定写 suspend service。

悬挂、吊挂；垂下

suspend sth from sth 动词+介词
She suspended the plant from the ceiling.
她把植物吊在天花板上。
suspend a lamp 动词搭配
They suspended a lamp over the table.
他们在桌子上方挂了一盏灯。
suspend from the ceiling 动词+介词
The decoration was suspended from the ceiling.
装饰品悬挂在天花板上。
suspend a picture 动词搭配
He suspended a picture on the wall.
他把一幅画挂在墙上。

中止、推迟；让进行中的事停下

suspend the plan 动词搭配

工程师再次核查设计期间，新线的投产被暂停。

The launch of the new line was suspended while engineers checked the design once more.
工程师再次核查设计期间，新线的投产被暂停。
suspend service 动词搭配
The launch of the new line was suspended while engineers checked the design once more.
工程师再次核查设计期间，新线的投产被暂停。
suspend a launch 动词搭配
The launch of the new line was suspended while engineers checked the design once more.
工程师再次核查设计期间，新线的投产被暂停。

停职、除名；暂时取消资格

be suspended from duty 系表搭配
Several players were suspended from competition while the investigation into the incident continued.
调查继续进行期间，几名球员被禁止参赛。
be suspended from school 系表搭配

调查继续进行期间，几名球员被禁止参赛。

调查继续进行期间，几名球员被禁止参赛。
suspended from play 动词+介词

调查继续进行期间，几名球员被禁止参赛。

使悬浮；（微粒）浮在空气中

remain suspended 动词搭配
Fine particles remain suspended in the air long after the spray is released.
喷雾释放后很久，细小颗粒仍悬浮在空气中。
suspended in the air 动词+介词

utter/ˈʌtə(r)/T2
考场判断法：① 先看词性：前面带冠词或不定冠词（an utter failure / utter nonsense），是形容词「完全的、彻底的」，不能译成「说出」；② 后面接 words / a cry / a warning / no sound，是动词「说出、发出」，注意 utter no sound 是「一声不吭」；③ 宾语是 doubt / opinion / complaint，是「表达、吐露」；④ 诗歌文学语境里的 utter 多半是「发出声音」的动作描写。写作里形容「胡言乱语」写 utter nonsense。

说出、开口讲（话、名字）

utter a word 动词搭配
She did not utter a word during the whole meeting.
整个会议期间她一句话也没说。
utter a name 动词搭配
He uttered her name quietly.
他轻声说出了她的名字。
utter no sound 动词搭配
The child uttered no sound all night.
这个孩子整夜没发出一点声音。
utter a greeting 动词搭配
She uttered a greeting as we passed by.
我们经过时她说了一声问候。

表达、吐露（想法、意见）

utter an opinion 动词搭配
She was careful never to utter a word of complaint, even when the work was exhausting.
即使工作累人，她也从不吐露一句抱怨。
utter a doubt 动词搭配
She was careful never to utter a word of complaint, even when the work was exhausting.
即使工作累人，她也从不吐露一句抱怨。
utter a complaint 动词搭配
She was careful never to utter a word of complaint, even when the work was exhausting.
即使工作累人，她也从不吐露一句抱怨。
utter no word of praise 动词+介词
She was careful never to utter a word of complaint, even when the work was exhausting.
即使工作累人，她也从不吐露一句抱怨。

发出（叫声、声音）

utter a cry 动词搭配
A faint cry was uttered somewhere in the dark, and everyone in the room fell silent.
黑暗中某处传来一声微弱的叫喊，屋里每个人都沉默了。
utter a scream 动词搭配
A faint cry was uttered somewhere in the dark, and everyone in the room fell silent.
黑暗中某处传来一声微弱的叫喊，屋里每个人都沉默了。
utter a sound 动词搭配
A faint cry was uttered somewhere in the dark, and everyone in the room fell silent.
黑暗中某处传来一声微弱的叫喊，屋里每个人都沉默了。
utter a groan 动词搭配
A faint cry was uttered somewhere in the dark, and everyone in the room fell silent.
黑暗中某处传来一声微弱的叫喊，屋里每个人都沉默了。

彻底的、绝顶的；毫无道理的

utter nonsense 动词搭配
His explanation sounded like utter nonsense, and no one in the room took it seriously.
他的解释听上去完全是胡说八道，屋里没人当真。
utter failure 动词搭配
His explanation sounded like utter nonsense, and no one in the room took it seriously.
他的解释听上去完全是胡说八道，屋里没人当真。
utter darkness 动词搭配
His explanation sounded like utter nonsense, and no one in the room took it seriously.
他的解释听上去完全是胡说八道，屋里没人当真。
utter stranger 动词搭配
His explanation sounded like utter nonsense, and no one in the room took it seriously.
他的解释听上去完全是胡说八道，屋里没人当真。
